- `AppModal` (M14) — a reusable, standardized modal primitive (`components/ui/app-modal.tsx`) built on the design-system `Dialog` (Radix): one `size` scale (sm/md/lg/xl) + a title/description/body/footer contract, delegating focus-trap/ESC/overlay/a11y to Radix. Becomes the system-wide pattern for transient action screens (replaces per-screen ad-hoc `Dialog.Content`).

- Wiki (`/rag/wiki`) action screens are now standardized modals (M14): New page moved out of the side pane into an `AppModal`; New folder (shared with the Workspace via `NewFolderDialog`) rebuilt on `AppModal`; Search stays the design-system CommandPalette. Reading/editing an existing page stays in the master-detail split.

Users create folders (a hidden `.keep` marker, like the Workspace) and create pages from inside the current folder (the page's path is `{folder}{title}`). Reading/editing a page and creating one open in a master-detail split — the browser collapses to a rail while the reader/editor takes the space — with the Cmd+K page palette and Read/Edit toggle preserved.

## [0.55.0] - 2026-07-18

### Added
- Storage (M4 CSP): the dashboard `connect-src` CSP now allowlists the storage endpoints (`https://storage.usetheo.dev`, `https://storage-dev.usetheo.dev`) so the browser can transfer bytes directly to/from object storage via presigned URLs (previously the CSP — a gate before CORS — blocked the direct request).
- Storage (M4 infra): `STORAGE_S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT` — a browser-reachable S3 host used ONLY to mint presigned URLs (the SigV4 signature binds the host), while the BFF keeps using the in-cluster `STORAGE_S3_ENDPOINT` for its own server-side ops. Empty falls back to the single endpoint (no behavior change for existing deploys).
- Storage (M4 workspace direct transfer): the Workspace uploads/downloads files **directly to/from object storage** via presigned URLs — the dashboard mints the URL, the browser transfers the bytes (no proxy through the BFF). Upload is mint → direct PUT → commit; download mints a short-lived GET. Requires a browser-reachable storage endpoint + bucket CORS (deploy note in docs/presigned-storage-roadmap.md).

### Changed

### Fixed

## [0.54.0] - 2026-07-18

### Added
- Storage (M2/M3 presigned data-plane): new BFF endpoints `POST /v1/…/storage/presign-upload`, `presign-commit`, and `presign-download`. Upload is **two-phase** — mint reserves quota + a pending row + a direct-to-S3 PUT URL; commit HEADs S3 to confirm the bytes landed **and match the declared size** (quota-bypass guard) before finalizing, releasing the reservation on a missing/mismatched upload (typed 409). Download mints a short-lived GET URL for a committed object. Both keep the tenant key server-derived (isolation) and take the path in the JSON body (avoids the `/objects/*` wildcard). Proxy-mode forwards all three to the storage upstream.

## [0.53.0] - 2026-07-18

### Added
- Storage (M1 presigned core): `s3x.Signer.Presign` + `Client.PresignPut/PresignGet` — query-string SigV4 presigned URLs so file bytes can flow **directly to/from S3** instead of through the BFF. Validated against an **independent SigV4 oracle** (byte-identical signature) + a real-MinIO round-trip integration test. Extends the `ObjectStore` DIP interface.

## [0.37.0] - 2026-07-17

### Added
- Knowledge Base (KB0): wiki page lifecycle — update (`PUT /v1/dashboard/rag/wiki/pages/{id}`, OKF-aware re-embed + rename/move via frontmatter path) and delete (`DELETE /v1/dashboard/rag/wiki/pages/{id}`) BFF proxies over theo-rag; unblocks the Notion-style editor (edit/rename/delete)
- RAG: chunk viewer + editor — open a document to see its chunks, edit a chunk's text, and save to regenerate its embedding (the corrected text becomes retrievable). New per-tenant BFF proxies (`GET /v1/dashboard/rag/documents/{id}/chunks`, `GET/PATCH /v1/dashboard/rag/chunks/{id}`) over theo-rag's chunk read + a new `PATCH /v1/chunks/{id}` mutation that re-embeds server-side

### Fixed
- RAG documents: the file-upload dropzone is now disabled with clear guidance ("Select a collection above to upload a file") until a collection is selected. Previously the dropzone was always active, so choosing a file with no collection selected hit a validation guard that silently discarded the file — the user then clicked upload and nothing happened. Now the required order (pick a collection, then drop a file) is enforced and obvious.
- Connectors: the PWA service worker no longer shadows the OAuth broker — "Connect with X" navigations to `/v1/rag-connect/*` now reach the backend instead of dead-ending at "Page not found" for users with a warm service worker (#203)
- Uploads (and every app action): fixed a service-worker crash (`ReferenceError` in the request router) that broke file upload. The worker's request matcher referenced a helper that Workbox did not bundle into the worker; it is now a self-contained pattern derived from the single-source denylist (#203 follow-up)

## [0.34.0] - 2026-07-16

### Added
- **M8 (Connectors page — "Connect a source" reaches real users) — the dashboard can now authorize an OAuth data source with no CLI.** `/rag/connectors` gains a "Connect a source" Dialog: pick a collection, then click "Connect with X", which is a **top-level navigation** (`<a href>` on the BFF origin) to the OAuth broker's `authorize` endpoint — the browser follows consent → callback → connect and lands back on `/rag/connectors?connected={provider}`, where a success banner confirms it. The dialog offers **only** the OAuth providers the operator actually registered, fetched from the new `GET /v1/dashboard/rag/connectors/providers` (returns the sorted providers whose `CLIENT_ID` is set; auth-gated 401), so a "Connect with X" button never dead-ends on a 422; providers are disabled until a collection is chosen. `internal/routes/rag_connectors_oauth.go` (`ListOAuthProviders` + 2 tests) + route wiring in `cmd/main.go` (rag route-set now 15) + `dashboard/src/pages/rag/connectors.tsx` (Dialog + Select + `Button asChild` anchors + `?connected=` banner; 3 new page tests, existing tests made query-order-robust). routes+rag+cmd-rag-route-set green, `go vet` + `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Completes the M8 human journey; final LIVE dev-public proof gated on the operator/sponsor Cleanroom+JUNTOS ceremony + real OAuth app registration. (usetheodev/theo-cloud#197)
- **M8 (OAuth authorization broker) — "Connect with X" consent flow at the edge, no hand-obtained refresh_token.** New `GET /v1/dashboard/rag/connectors/{provider}/authorize` + `.../callback` broker the full OAuth 2.0 Authorization Code + PKCE (S256) dance server-side: `authorize` mints a tenant-bound CSRF `state` + PKCE verifier/challenge, stores them in three HttpOnly/Secure/SameSite=Lax cookies (TTL 600s, mirroring the GitHub-integrations broker), and 302-redirects to the provider consent screen; `callback` verifies `state` param == cookie (CSRF → 403), exchanges `code`+verifier for a `refresh_token` **server-to-server** (the `client_secret` never leaves the edge), then reuses the M8 connect proxy to POST `{collection_id, refresh_token}` and redirects to the dashboard. The whole flow is proven against a stub `TokenExchanger` (sandbox stand-in — no real OAuth app needed): 7 broker tests (authorize redirect + PKCE/state/cookies, unknown/non-OAuth provider 422, missing collection 422, unauthed 401, callback happy-path connect+redirect, CSRF-mismatch 403, exchange-fails 502). Providers are **operator-gated**: `LoadOAuthConnectorsFromEnv` offers a provider only once its `THEO_RAG_OAUTH_<PROVIDER>_CLIENT_ID/_CLIENT_SECRET` is registered (unconfigured → 422), so no half-configured consent link is ever shown. `HTTPTokenExchanger` is the production default. `internal/routes/rag_connectors_oauth.go` + wiring in `cmd/main.go`; routes+rag+cmd-rag-route-set green, `go vet` clean. Final LIVE dev-public proof gated on the operator/sponsor Cleanroom+JUNTOS ceremony (OAuth app registration is a second operator gate). (usetheodev/theo-cloud#197)
- **M8 (BFF connect proxy) — the dashboard BFF can now CONNECT a data source, not just list/sync/delete.** New `POST /v1/dashboard/rag/connectors/{provider}/connect` proxies to theo-rag: the `{provider}` path param is validated fail-fast against the 12+2 connector enum (unknown → typed 422, never forwarded), and the credential body (refresh_token / static creds / crawler config) is forwarded **opaquely** — the BFF never inspects, transforms, or logs it (Rule 8), only the provider name is logged. `DataClient.ConnectConnector` + handler + route + 2 tests (opaque-body forward / unknown-provider-422 fail-fast); routes+rag+cmd rag route-set green, go vet clean. Foundation for the M8 OAuth broker + Connectors page. (usetheodev/theo-cloud#197)
- **M9 — GraphRAG reachable in the Ask UI (strategy selector).** `AskInput` gains `Strategy` (omitempty — unset request is byte-identical to pre-M9); `POST /v1/dashboard/rag/answers` validates it fail-fast against the enum `vector|keyword|hybrid|graph|global` (unknown → typed 422, never forwarded) and passes valid/empty through, unlocking GraphRAG (`graph`/`global`) from the dashboard threaded from a new dashboard selector. 3 BFF tests (forward / invalid-422-fail-fast / empty-backward-compat) + full routes+rag packages green; `go vet` clean. The dashboard `ask.tsx` gains an outcome-labelled strategy `Select` (Fast/Keyword/Hybrid/Entity graph/Global sensemaking; default Fast omits the field so the request is byte-identical to pre-M9) and surfaces the answering strategy + honest router fallback when theo-rag returns a trace. 6 tests (3 BFF + 3 page) + tsc + eslint + go vet clean. `internal/rag/data_client.go` + `internal/routes/rag_dashboard.go` + `dashboard/src/pages/rag/ask.tsx`. (usetheodev/theo-cloud#198)
- Roadmap amended: added **M8 RAG Connectors reach real users (dashboard page + BFF proxy + OAuth authorization broker)** (`/roadmap-feature rag-connectors-ux-oauth-broker`). Turns theo-rag's built-and-LIVE-proven 12-provider connectors platform (theo-rag#97) into a real human journey — subsumes theo-cloud#191 (page+proxy) and adds the missing OAuth authorization broker at the edge ("Connect with X" consent → callback → code→refresh_token → connect). (Note: `M8` here is the macro ROADMAP.md sequence, distinct from the `M8`-`M12` observability numbering used by the lens module handoff doc.)
- Roadmap amended: added **M9 GraphRAG reachable in the Ask UI (strategy selector)** (`/roadmap-feature graphrag-in-ask-ui`). Surfaces theo-rag's already-shipped `strategy: global|graph|hybrid` (Microsoft-GraphRAG-style global sensemaking) in `ask.tsx`, which today only calls the default `vector`.
- **M12 — the lens labeling-queue resolve form now composes `@usetheo/ui@0.26` `AnnotationInput` over config-driven score-configs.** The bespoke free-form label/score/note inputs on the resolve Sheet are replaced by named, config-driven `AnnotationInput`s: a **required free-text "Label"** (freeform — preserves the prior free-text capability so reviewers are never forced into fixed buckets), a categorical **"Quality"** (good→1, ok→0.5, bad→0) that maps a named choice to the numeric `score`, and a freeform **"Note"**. Because theo-cloud has NO backend score-config store yet, the score-configs live CLIENT-SIDE as a FIXTURE (`dashboard/src/pages/lens/labeling-queue/score-configs.ts`) — the ROADMAP-sanctioned M12 mitigation, seeded so a future backend store becomes a drop-in. The `POST …/labeling-queue/{id}/resolve` body is unchanged (`{label, score?, note?}`): a new PURE, unit-tested boundary `toResolveBody` (+ `prefillValues` for the edit path) assembles it — a selected categorical option maps to `{label, score}`, the freeform Note maps to `{note}`, empties are omitted (same `trim()`/finite-number semantics as before), and a bucket-only review falls back to the option label so the required `label` is never empty. Loading/error/optimistic behavior and the submit endpoint are untouched. 23 labeling-queue tests green (14 mapper unit tests incl. round-trip through `prefillValues`; 9 page tests incl. a categorical-bucket→score wiring test); tsc + eslint clean; no new npm dependency. `dashboard/src/pages/lens/labeling-queue.tsx` + `labeling-queue/{score-configs,to-resolve-body,resolve-form}.ts(x)`.
- **M11 — analytics time-series charts on the lens dashboards board (adopt `@usetheo/ui@0.26` `Histogram` + `PercentileChart`).** The board now renders a **trace-duration distribution histogram** (its own `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces` read through `cloudFetch`, `durationMs` binned client-side into the design system's `Histogram`; a trace with no finite `durationMs` is dropped, never binned as a fabricated 0). The board's traces window carries its own loading (skeleton) / error (Alert) / empty (the Histogram's own "no data" figure) states. New pure, unit-tested `durationsFromTraces` boundary (drops absent / NaN / ±Infinity). `dashboard/src/pages/lens/dashboards/duration-histogram.tsx`.
- **M9 — session detail page (`/observability/sessions/:sessionId`).** A new dashboard screen shows, for one session, the aggregate metrics (total duration/window, ∑ cost, ∑ tokens, error count, trace count, distinct models) via `@usetheo/ui` `SessionSummary` and the temporal replay (one row per trace, sorted by start time, duration bars) via `SessionTimeline`. It reuses the existing traces read (`GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces?session_id=<id>` through `cloudFetch`) and maps each traces-list row to the design system's generic `SessionTraceItem` via a pure, unit-tested boundary mapper (`session-detail/to-session-item.ts` — computes `endTime = start + durationMs` in ns, collapses the OTLP status vocabulary to OK/ERROR, honestly nulls absent values), mirroring the M8 `to-trace-span` precedent. The sessions list row now offers "Open session" (the detail) alongside the existing "View traces" jump. Honest loading (skeleton) / error (Alert) / empty (EmptyState) states; back link to the sessions list. 6 page tests + 11 mapper unit tests green; tsc + eslint clean. `dashboard/src/pages/lens/session-detail.tsx` + `session-detail/to-session-item.ts`.

### Changed
- **RAG OAuth connectors: provider `AUTHORIZE_URL`/`TOKEN_URL` are now env-overridable** (`THEO_RAG_OAUTH_<PROVIDER>_AUTHORIZE_URL`/`_TOKEN_URL`). A legitimate testability / self-hosted-IdP feature: staging can point a provider at a conformant OAuth2 server (e.g. Ory Hydra) to exercise the full authorize→consent→callback→token-exchange loop LIVE without a real third-party app. URLs come from env (operator), never request input — no new SSRF surface; absent env, the well-known public defaults stand. 2 tests (override + defaults-preserved).
- **M11 — lens dashboards latency overlay migrated to a banded `PercentileChart`.** The `p95_latency` trend widget on the dashboards board now renders p50/p95/p99 as shaded BANDS (`@usetheo/ui` `PercentileChart`) instead of three overlaid flat lines — the spread is read at a glance. A new pure, unit-tested mapper `latencyPercentileBuckets(LatencyBucket[]) → PercentileBucket[]` (in `lens-series.ts`) does the `{p50,p95,p99,label}` mapping with a UTC `HH:MM` bucket label (falls back to the raw `periodStart` when it is not a parseable ISO instant — no fabricated time). `bindTrend` no longer routes `p95_latency` (the widget special-cases it into the band); the 3-line `latencySeries` overlay remains only on the separate `/observability` Overview page (`lens.tsx`, out of scope). 16 mapper + 10 widget + 17 dashboards-page tests green; tsc + eslint clean.

### Fixed
- **M8 Connectors page: render the `?connect_error=<reason>` banner (found during LIVE dev-public test).** The broker's L4 fix (#199) redirects `Callback` failures to `/rag/connectors?connect_error=…`, but the page only read `?connected=` — so a failed connect returned the user to the page with no visual feedback. It now reads `connect_error` too and shows a typed destructive Alert (per-reason human message: unknown_provider / missing_collection / state_mismatch / context_expired / provider_exchange_failed / connect_failed / internal), mirroring the success banner + stripping the query param. 8/8 connectors page tests + tsc + eslint clean. Surfaced by the dev-public browser walkthrough (screenshot showed `?connect_error=unknown_provider` in the URL with no banner).
- **M8 OAuth broker: browser-redirect endpoints now use Kratos session-cookie auth (was Bearer-JWT) — closes the review-caught BLOCKER (usetheodev/theo-cloud#199).** `Authorize`/`Callback` were mounted under the Bearer-only `requireJWT`, but they are browser top-level redirects that cannot carry an `Authorization` header → every real callback would 401 in prod (or leak the tenant JWT to the provider if worked around). They are now mounted at the **root path `/v1/rag-connect/{provider}/{authorize,callback}`** (no `requireJWT`) and authenticated by the **Kratos session cookie** via a new fail-closed `KratosSessionTenantResolver` (cookie → `/sessions/whoami` → `EnsureTenant`), mirroring the GitHub-App integration. The `Callback` runs the server-to-server connect **as the cookie-resolved tenant** (`auth.WithUser` wraps the context so the existing per-tenant key resolver applies) — the tenant comes from the session, never from a browser-supplied value, and the `refresh_token` still never reaches the browser. Also folded in from the same review: `redirectBase` defaults to the **BFF origin** (new `CLOUD_PUBLIC_URL` / `cfg.CloudPublicURL`, not the dashboard origin) so the callback + its CSRF/PKCE cookies land on the right host in split-subdomain deployments; CSRF `state` uses `subtle.ConstantTimeCompare`; the CSRF/PKCE cookies are scoped to `Path=/v1/rag-connect` (was `/`); `Authorize`/`Callback` gate on a **registered** provider (`ClientID != ""`, matching the advertised list); and `Callback` failures **redirect to `/rag/connectors?connect_error=…`** (a browser banner) instead of a raw JSON blob. `ListOAuthProviders` stays a Bearer API (the dashboard fetches it). New tests: 4 resolver fail-closed cases + callback-unauthed (asserts connect NOT called + redirect to login) + CSRF-not-called. `dashboard/src/pages/rag/connectors.tsx` `authorizeHref` retargeted to `/v1/rag-connect`. Independent re-review verdict: **READY_TO_MERGE**. Go 26/26 packages + `go vet` + `tsc` + `eslint` clean. Final LIVE proof still gated on the operator/sponsor Cleanroom+JUNTOS ceremony + OAuth app registration.
- **`develop` is green again — two release-blocking `go test ./...` failures resolved (unblocks the develop→main cut).** (1) `TestLensDashboard_ReadWriteRouteSet` failed on the M63 `POST …/traces/{traceID}/enqueue-for-labeling` route (ADR-3 read-only-surface invariant): enqueue-for-labeling adds a span to a labeling-review queue — a sanctioned management write that never mutates the immutable trace body, the same category as `/tags` / `/annotations` / `/replay` — so it is now in the test's `writableMeta` allowlist (usetheodev/theo-cloud#169). (2) `tests/unit` failed to compile: `fakeTheMemory.Graph` had the pre-`includeSuperseded` 3-arg signature while the `TheMemoryClient` interface + real client + caller all take the 4-arg `Graph(ctx, userID, scope, includeSuperseded bool)` — the stale test fake is updated to match (no production change). Full suite now 26/26 packages green, `go vet` clean.

## [0.33.0] - 2026-07-15

### Added
- **RAG Analytics — per-collection scale observability rendered in the dashboard (delegation `m7-scale-ui` T3).** The `/rag/analytics` page gains a collection picker; when the operator selects a collection the BFF forwards `?collection_id=X` to theo-rag rc.80+, decodes the returned `scale` block (extraction tokens, graph communities total/max_level/per_level, corpus documents/chunks/size_bytes) and renders it as a second card next to the M5 summary. Backward-compat: no collection picked → the request stays on the M5 shape (no `scale` in response, no scale card). BFF passthrough is a pure widening — `RagDataClient.Analytics` now takes `(ctx, collectionID, since)`; `AnalyticsResult.Scale *ScaleObservability` uses `omitempty` so unrelated `/v1/dashboard/rag/analytics` consumers see zero shape drift. Hermetic tests: `TestRagDataClient_Analytics_ForwardsCollectionID_DecodesScale` (client wires `?collection_id=` and decodes every `scale.*` sub-field), `TestRagDashboard_Analytics_ForwardsCollectionID_PropagatesScale` + `TestRagDashboard_Analytics_NoCollectionID_NoScale` (handler propagates and preserves backward-compat), Vitest `after picking a collection, fetches with collection_id and renders the scale block`. `internal/rag/data_client.go`, `internal/routes/rag_dashboard.go`, `dashboard/src/pages/rag/analytics.tsx`.
- **Workspace bulk operations — multi-select + folder delete, backend (plan `workspace-bulk-operations`, Phase 1).** New `POST /v1/dashboard/storage/bulk-delete` deletes a set of files (`paths[]`) and/or whole folders (`prefixes[]`) in one tenant-scoped call, returning `{deleted, freed_bytes, failed[], truncated}`. It composes a shared `DeletePrefixOver` loop used by BOTH the in-process `Service` and the thin-proxy `Client` (identical behavior in either storage mode): deletes every object under a folder prefix, bounded by `bulkDeleteMaxObjects` (10000, `truncated` beyond, D3); partial-failure-aware — a per-object failure is a `failed[]` row + HTTP 200, never a 5xx nor an aborted batch (D4); idempotent — an already-absent object is success, not a failure (D5, so overlapping selections / re-clicks / cross-tab deletes are safe). `safePrefix` rejects empty / whole-tenant (`/`, `.`, `..`) / non-trailing-slash prefixes so a mass-delete can never substring-match (`READ`→`README.md`) nor wipe a tenant (EC-2/EC-3); the tenant comes from the session, never the client (EC-1). New `theo_storage_objects_deleted_total` counter. Frontend: the Workspace page gains row checkboxes + select-all (indeterminate), a selection action bar, a per-folder Delete affordance, and a ConfirmDialog that shows the blast radius (item count + total size, "at least N" when the listing is paginated) before deleting; partial failure keeps exactly the failed items selected for retry (EC-5) and surfaces honestly via a toast. Selection logic + the preview panel + the new-folder dialog were extracted into `use-workspace-selection`, `workspace-preview-sheet`, and `new-folder-dialog` to keep `workspace.tsx` under the size budget. All Go + 13 vitest tests green; tsc + eslint clean; binary builds.
- **M7 storage dashboard — upload control (closes a Dashboard-UI DoD gap found during LIVE browser validation).** The Storage page had list + per-file share/delete + a quota bar, but NO way to upload a file from the browser — the M7 DoD explicitly requires upload in the dashboard UI, and the BFF already exposed `PUT /v1/dashboard/storage/objects/*`; only the frontend control was missing. Added an "Upload files" control — a native `<label>` wrapping a multi-file `<input>` (rendered with the Button's styles via `asChild`), so clicking it opens the OS file picker natively (the first cut used `display:none` + `<Button onClick=ref.click()>`, which silently no-op'd on real user clicks — a scripted `.click()` outside a user-gesture never opens the picker). It PUTs each file's raw bytes with its own `Content-Type` to the existing BFF route, refreshes the list + quota on success, and surfaces typed plan-limit rejections in product language ("larger than your plan allows" on 413, "reached your plan's storage limit" on 402). 7/7 vitest GREEN (incl. PUT-shape, 413 rejection, and a label-association regression guard), tsc + eslint clean. `dashboard/src/pages/storage.tsx`.
- Published `@usetheo/storage@0.1.0` to npm (public) — the M7 storage TypeScript SDK.
- **M7 tenant blob storage — Go API vertical COMPLETE (engineering; LIVE-gated):** the `/v1/storage/*` vertical (upload/download/list/stat/delete/share + unauthenticated share-redemption), accessed via existing `theo_live_*` keys with new `storage:read`/`storage:write` scopes; per-tier quota (402 aggregate / 413 per-file, Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB) via reserve→commit→compensate with `pg_advisory_xact_lock`; fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 (validated against pocketbase vectors, zero aws-sdk); HMAC share tokens (constant-time); server-derived `{tenant}/{path}` S3 keys (EC-1 cross-tenant isolation) + `nosniff`+`attachment` on download/redemption (EC-2 stored-XSS); Prometheus `theo_storage_bytes_written_total` metering; engine migration `000057_storage_objects`. Wired into `cmd/main.go` (enabled when `STORAGE_S3_*` configured). All `go test -race` GREEN; full binary builds. Blueprint SHIPPABLE 100.0 + plan SHIPPABLE 95.6. Remaining M7 scope: dashboard page + `theo storage` CLI + `@usetheo/storage` TS SDK + dogfood journey; LIVE validation + checkbox flip operator-gated (ADR D5).
- **M7 tenant blob storage (superseded line — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- Roadmap amended: added M7 Tenant Blob Storage — managed file storage, complete solution across API `/v1/storage/*` + CLI + SDK + dashboard UI + dogfood journeys (`/roadmap-feature tenant-blob-storage`; deps M1+M3; product grill + SHIPPABLE blueprint referenced in the milestone block)
- **Dashboard: RAG Connectors page + BFF proxy (#191):** a new `/rag/connectors` page lists the workspace's connector connections (provider / collection / status / created) and lets a user trigger a sync or delete one (confirm); backed by 3 new tenant-gated BFF proxy routes (`GET /v1/dashboard/rag/connectors`, `POST .../{id}/sync`, `DELETE .../{id}`) mirroring the collections surface (Model-B per-tenant key). v1 is MANAGE-only — connecting a NEW source from the dashboard (per-provider OAuth/credential flow) + the documents-page enrichment + a webhooks page remain follow-ups on #191.


### Changed
- **ROADMAP M7 note revised (2026-07-14) — architecture pivot documented + fresh re-verification.** The M7 milestone note now records the pivot to the separate `theo-storage` data-plane service + thin-proxy mode, and the two valid LIVE paths for the checkbox flip (in-process on dev-public vs. separate service behind the edge). Re-verified GREEN this session (local Docker): theo-cli 9/9, dashboard 4/4, `@usetheo/storage` SDK 6/6 against the live standalone service, theo-storage domain+glue, theo-cloud in-process integration (real PG+MinIO), and the proxy strangler-fig E2E. Engineering DoD 100% evidenced; checkbox remains `[ ]` pending the LIVE dev-public ceremony (Cleanroom + Red Team). Handoff refreshed at `.claude/knowledge-base/handoff-2026-07-14-m7-storage-live.md`.
- **M7 storage — thin-proxy mode (strangler-fig to the standalone `theo-storage` service):** the `/v1/storage/*` vertical now runs in one of two interchangeable modes behind the same `routes.StorageService` interface (DIP). Setting `STORAGE_UPSTREAM_URL` switches theo-cloud to a **thin proxy** that forwards each op to the standalone `theo-storage` data-plane service, injecting the resolved tenant as `X-Theo-Tenant` (theo-cloud is the trusted edge — it already authenticated the client's `theo_live_*` key + scope, exactly like the Model B ForwardAuth boundary the other data services use). When `STORAGE_UPSTREAM_URL` is unset the in-process S3-backed vertical is used as before (no behavior change). New `internal/storageproxy` package maps upstream status codes back to the same typed errors so the handler's error mapping is identical in both modes. Proven end-to-end (`-tags=integration`): real StorageHandler → real proxy → real header-mode `theo-storage` → real PG+MinIO, full chain with EC-2 headers + typed errors surviving the proxy hop.
- **Storage → Workspace: the dashboard storage page is now a polished folder-browser at `/workspaces`.** Renamed the "Storage" nav entry + page to "Workspace" (route `/storage` now redirects to `/workspaces`), and replaced the flat file list with a hierarchical file manager modeled on TheoKit Studio's Workspaces: navigable folders, a breadcrumb + workspace-root button, upload-into-the-current-folder, refresh, per-file share/delete, and a subtle plan-usage indicator. The tenant store (M7) is flat, so folders are derived from the `/` in each object's path (`deriveEntries`, unit-tested); an empty folder is materialized with a hidden `.keep` marker. Fully in-app interactions (no native `window.prompt`): **New folder** is an in-app `Dialog` with an `Input`; clicking a file opens a **preview side-panel** (`Sheet`) that renders text/code (`CodeBlock`) and images inline with metadata + Download / Share (`CopyButton`) / Delete; deletion goes through a `ConfirmDialog`. Product language only. 9/9 vitest GREEN (folder grouping, subfolder navigation, upload-into-prefix, in-app new-folder dialog asserting `window.prompt` is never called, file-preview panel, no-infra-terms); tsc + eslint + build clean. `dashboard/src/pages/workspace.tsx` (replaces `storage.tsx`).


### Fixed
- **Dashboard SPA rendered blank from a production build — `Cannot access 'X' before initialization` (TDZ) — so the fresh bundle never actually ran (found deploying the Workspace UI, 2026-07-15).** The `manualChunks` vendor split gave `@usetheo/ui`, `@radix-ui`, and `lucide-react` dedicated chunks, but `@usetheo/ui` re-exports Radix + utility deps that also land in the generic `vendor` chunk — forming a `vendor ↔ theo-ui ↔ radix` chunk cycle. Rollup then ordered a `vendor` binding read before its initialization → TDZ → blank SPA. The same failure mode ADR D1 already documents for `scheduler`+`react` (circular chunks must be MERGED, not split). Fixed by dropping the `theo-ui`/`radix`/`lucide` dedicated chunks (they fall to the single `vendor` chunk, breaking the cycle) and keeping the `react-force-graph`/`d3` family OUT of every eager chunk so it rides the already-`React.lazy` `graph` chunk (returning `undefined` from `manualChunks` for that family — a dedicated chunk just moved the circular boundary to `vendor ↔ force-graph`). Verified LIVE: the SPA mounts, the console is clean, and the lazy `/memory/graph` page (force-graph consumer) loads without TDZ. `manual-chunks.test.ts` updated (8 cases incl. the force-graph family + d3-lookalike guards); build emits zero `Circular chunk` warnings. `dashboard/src/build/manual-chunks.ts`.
- **Dashboard `vite build` was broken — the served bundle was stale, so new UI (e.g. Workspace multi-select) never shipped (found deploying the Workspace bulk-delete UI, 2026-07-15).** `@usetheo/ui@0.22` (upstream regression #10) stopped publishing the CSS assets `dashboard/src/index.css` imported (`@usetheo/ui/preset.css`, `/components.css`, `/fonts-cdn.css`) — the package migrated to a JS Tailwind-v4 preset and no longer ships those `.css` specifiers, so `vite build` failed with `Missing "./preset.css" specifier in "@usetheo/ui"` for **every** consumer of the dashboard. The `@usetheo/ui` bump that introduced this validated the vitest suite (1734 green) but never ran `vite build`, so the breakage went unnoticed and the deployed dist was stale. Rather than downgrade `@usetheo/ui` 8 versions (which breaks the component APIs the whole dashboard uses), vendored the canonical token layers (`tokens.css` + `tokens-v4.css` + `fonts-cdn.css`, taken verbatim from the design system's own Ladle preview) under `dashboard/src/vendor/usetheo-ui/` and let Tailwind scan the package's compiled components via `@source '../node_modules/@usetheo/ui/dist'`. Build is green again; the Violet Forge theme renders unchanged. See `dashboard/src/vendor/usetheo-ui/README.md` for provenance + the removal path when #10 is fixed upstream.
- **Empty (0-byte) objects were rejected with 503 — empty files (`__init__.py`, `.gitkeep`, placeholders) could not be stored (found via a real `theo storage sync` of a `.claude/`, 2026-07-15).** The S3 write path built the PUT with an arbitrary non-nil body reader (the `http.MaxBytesReader` the Upload handler streams) and a 0-length `Content-Length`; Go cannot know the length of an arbitrary reader, so it emitted chunked `Transfer-Encoding`, which S3/MinIO rejects under SigV4 single-payload signing → surfaced as `ErrStorageUnavailable` (503). Go only auto-normalizes `*bytes`/`*strings.Reader` of length 0 to `http.NoBody`; the real path does not use those. Now `s3x.Client.PutObject` (in-process mode) and `storageproxy.Client.Upload` (thin-proxy mode) both force `http.NoBody` when `size == 0`, so empty objects PUT with a clean `Content-Length: 0` and an empty-payload signature. This is the true fix for the same 0-byte rejection that had forced the dashboard folder-marker to use a 1-byte `.keep`. Regression test `TestClient_PutObject_EmptyBody` uses an arbitrary reader (`io.NopCloser`) to reproduce the chunked emission and asserts `Content-Length: 0` + no chunked. `internal/storage/s3x/client.go` + `internal/storageproxy/client.go`.
- **Workspace file preview/download returned 405 — the dashboard storage BFF never mounted the download route (found via LIVE per-agent sync test, 2026-07-15).** The `/v1/dashboard/storage` browser surface mounted List / Stat / Upload / Delete / Share but NOT `GET /objects/*` (download) — the sibling `theo_live_*` `/v1/storage` surface had it, but the dashboard never needed download until the new folder-browser preview. A GET to a nested object path therefore matched the PUT/DELETE-only `/objects/*` pattern and chi returned `405 Method Not Allowed`, so the Workspace preview panel showed "Could not load this file" and Download failed. Extracted the mount into a testable `StorageHandler.MountDashboardRoutes` (the composition root now delegates to it) and added `GET /objects/*` → the existing, EC-2-hardened `Download` handler (`attachment` + `nosniff` preserved). Regression test `TestStorageHandler_DashboardMount_DownloadNestedObject` exercises the real mount and asserts a nested GET returns 200, not 405. `internal/routes/storage.go` + `cmd/main.go`.
- **Register flow was fully broken (503, then silent no-op) — two bugs, both fixed (found via theo-memory dogfood J-UI-10, 2026-07-12):** (1) the register BFF proxy (`internal/routes/register_proxy.go`) followed the upstream identity-store's 303 redirect to its public browser URL (`localhost:8090`), which from inside the gateway container targets its own loopback with nothing listening → "connection refused" → a wrong `503 Service temporarily unavailable`; the proxy's HTTP client now sets `CheckRedirect: http.ErrUseLastResponse` so it captures the redirect and lets the real browser follow it. (2) the dashboard register form (`dashboard/src/pages/register.tsx`) posted to `/v1/auth/register` WITHOUT the `?flow=<id>` query param, so the upstream rejected the submit and the anti-enumeration unifier silently mapped it to the check-email response with NO account created; the form action now carries the flow id. Regression tests: `register_proxy_test.go` `TestRegisterProxy_UpstreamRedirect_NotFollowed_Returns303` + `register.test.tsx` flow-id assertion. Register → login → dashboard now works end-to-end from a fresh browser.
- Dashboard smoke test now actually type-checks the dashboard: added the missing `typecheck` npm script (`tsc --noEmit`) that the `task test:smoke` target already invoked — previously it fell through to a graceful "not configured" echo, silently skipping the dashboard type-check (a false-green). Closes the T2.6 DoD of canonical-tests-rewrite-v2 ("dashboard type-check clean"); tsc is clean (0 errors).

## [0.32.1] - 2026-07-14

### Fixed
- **theo-cloud#193 — billing "USAGE THIS PERIOD" showed Dec 31, 1969 (epoch-0) on Pro plan.** Red Team QA session 2026-07-14T13:xx flagged: after Stripe upgrade to Pro, dashboard `/billing` renders `USAGE THIS PERIOD: DEC 31, 1969 – DEC 31, 1969`. Root cause: Stripe API 2025 relocated `current_period_start/end` from subscription root to `items.data[].current_period_start/end` (per-item billing periods). The `handleSubscriptionUpdated` handler in `internal/billing/webhook.go:231-247` reads from root only → `json.Unmarshal` leaves int64 zero → `time.Unix(0,0)=1970-01-01` → dashboard TZ-adjusts to "Dec 31, 1969". LIVE evidence pre-fix: `SELECT current_period_start FROM subscriptions WHERE tenant_id='tn-d10e0b6430d51588'` = `1970-01-01 00:00:00+00`. Confirmed via `GET https://api.stripe.com/v1/subscriptions/{id}` — actual periods live at `items.data[0].current_period_start=1784036010` (2026-07-14) + `items.data[0].current_period_end=1786714410` (2026-08-14). Canonical fix: add `CurrentPeriodStart/End` fields to `sub.Items.Data[]` struct + prefer item-level values when non-zero, fall back to root (legacy pre-2025 fixtures). Backfilled current tenant PG row LIVE. NO workaround (no time.Now() fallback per Rule 3 honesty — dashboard should render "—" on zero, not hide with current-time deception; that's a separate dashboard hardening ticket).

## [0.30.0] - 2026-07-10

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Dashboard UX polish (M98 — closes theo-data v9).** The last craft gaps on the observability screens: (1) the trace **peek-sheet** lazy-loads the trace-detail view (CodeMirror + react-markdown) behind `<Suspense>` — a separate ~53 kB chunk off the traces first-paint, loaded only on peek-open — and the sanitized `Markdown` wrapper is extracted to its own module so the Ask thread no longer bundles CodeMirror; (2) the alerts **On/Off toggle** flips **optimistically** with `onError` rollback + disabled-while-pending (no stale RTT gap, no silent divergence on a failed PATCH); (3) the experiments **compare** view shows a per-lane loading Skeleton (no fabricated-empty during load); (4) the traces table's **columns build + client sort are memoized** with stable deps (no rebuild per filter keystroke); (5) the hand-rolled **columns popover** gains Escape/outside-click/focus management + `useId` a11y ids; (6) the 4× duplicated lens `errorMessage` helper is DRY'd into one `lensErrorMessage`. Behavior-preserving — the full dashboard suite (1744 tests) is green. Adversarial review READY_TO_MERGE after it caught (and fixed) a defeated `useMemo` whose unstable deps made the memoization claim untrue.

## [0.29.0] - 2026-07-09

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Dynamic attribute filtering on the traces list (M92, theo-cloud half).** The traces filter bar gains an **attribute** facet: pick/type any instrumented span-attribute key (with native `<datalist>` suggestions from a workspace-scoped facet-discovery read) + a value, and the list narrows to spans whose `attributes ->> key` equals it — breaking the hard-coded 4-field filter set (Langfuse/Phoenix parity). It serializes to a `span_query=attr:key=value` DSL clause the BFF forwards to theo-lens (which binds the key as a jsonb path — injection-safe); the chip round-trips through the URL and shows the human-facing `key=value`. A failed/empty facet discovery degrades to a free-text key input (still usable). The engine DSL + facet endpoint ship in theo-lens.
- **Score-over-time on the Evaluators page (M90, theo-cloud half).** A range-selectable (`1h|24h|7d|30d`) mean-score trend chart answering "is eval quality trending up or down?" — one line per numeric evaluator over the selected window, reusing the M51/M60 `MetricTrendChart` (no new dep). A BFF proxy (`GET /v1/dashboard/lens/metrics/scores`) forwards the whitelisted range + the per-tenant key to theo-lens `GET /v1/metrics/scores`; an engine failure maps to a 502 `UPSTREAM_ERROR` (never a fabricated empty body). Categorical-only evaluators are not plotted on a mean axis; an empty range renders an honest "No evaluations yet" (never a fabricated flat-zero line — M89/M90 data-honesty); a scores backend failure surfaces a fail-closed error Alert (no chart). The engine aggregation half ships in theo-lens.



### Changed
- **Unified the headline-KPI primitive on `MetricCard` (#138, M88).** The dashboard had two primitives for the same "headline KPI" concept — `StatTile` on the memory/rag landing pages, `MetricCard` on every observability (lens) surface. All KPI tiles now use `MetricCard` (the house convention, richer + already standard across the lens module); `StatTile` is removed from the dashboard (a recurrence test guards it). The migrated memory/rag KPIs are visually equivalent (title + value + icon). Genuine page/section no-data states (Experiments "No runs yet", the Guards breaches sheet, the Memory webhooks deliveries sheet) now render the shared `EmptyState`; small widget-cell / control-adjacent hints stay inline by design (not every "no data yet" is a full empty-state card).



### Fixed
- **Integration-coverage backfill on the v7 surfaces (M91).** Test-only. Closes the named gaps the v8 audit (C6) flagged so a silently-broken vote or delete is caught: the copilot answer feedback now covers the **down**-vote + **up→down switch** (the POST carries the new label; no stale pressed state) and the **failed-retract** path (a rejected clear-DELETE reverts to the *prior* label, never a cleared thumb that did not persist — review H-1); a span-grounded turn's feedback now asserts the `spanId` is carried in the POST body (a previously dead test branch); the observability-context webhooks now cover the **delete** flow end-to-end (confirm-phrase gate → `DELETE /v1/settings/webhooks/{id}`) plus the **fail-closed negative** (a rejected delete leaves the webhook listed — no optimistic removal without rollback). No update-flow test — webhooks are immutable (rotate = delete + recreate). Each new negative was mutation-checked (verified to fail against a deliberately broken revert).
- **`MetricTrendChart`'s screen-reader table no longer mis-attributes ragged-series values (M90).** The visually-hidden a11y data table indexed every series positionally off `series[0].points`, which is only correct for equal-length (dense) series. M90's score-trend is the first ragged input (an evaluator with a real period gap has fewer points), so a sparse series' value was read under the wrong period column for AT users. The table now keys each cell by the shared period axis (the sorted union of all series' x-values); a period a series has no point for renders "—". Dense charts are unchanged.
- **Never-measured token counts no longer render a fabricated "0 tok" (M89).** The playground baseline column summed `(inputTokens ?? 0) + (outputTokens ?? 0)`, so a span whose token counts were never recorded showed "0 tok" — indistinguishable from a real zero-token call. It now renders "—" when *both* halves are absent, while a genuinely-measured `0` (or a partial where one half was recorded) still shows the real value. The trace-tree row applies the same honesty: the absent half of a partial measurement renders "—" (e.g. `100→—`) instead of a fabricated `100→0`. Mirrors the memory/rag "no fabricated zeros" discipline (an absence is not a zero).
- **Alert bodies no longer silently vanish across the dashboard (#175, M87).** The `@usetheo/ui` `Alert` renders only `title`/`description`/`action` and **silently dropped** any body passed as `children` — 42 sites across 15 memory/rag/api-keys pages were shipping title-only error notices (the body was invisible). Every `Alert` in the dashboard (45 files) now routes through the `@/components/ui/alert-guard` wrapper, which forwards a `children` body into `description` so it is always shown AND announced. A new `no-restricted-imports` ESLint rule bans importing the raw `@usetheo/ui` `Alert` in feature code (the guard is the only sanctioned consumer), so a #175-class regression is now a **lint error**. Kit-side, the going-forward `@usetheo/ui` line (published 0.15.0) additionally rejects `children` at the type level on its six fixed-slot primitives (a compile error). Note: the dashboard pins the stale `@usetheo/ui@0.13.x` line (superseded, incompatible with 0.15.0's dropped theming/billing exports) — migrating it onto the hardened line is a tracked follow-up.

## [0.28.0] - 2026-07-08

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Webhook management inside the Observability module (theo-lens M86).** Webhook registration is now reachable from within observability — a dedicated **`/observability/webhooks`** route (sidebar entry under *Improve*, next to Alerts) rendering the **exact same** webhooks CRUD (list / create / delete / deliveries) as Settings › Webhooks, via the existing `/v1/settings/webhooks/*` endpoints (reuse-first — the same `WorkspaceWebhooksPage` component, no duplication, no theo-lens change). The Alerts "register a webhook first" prerequisite now links to `/observability/webhooks` instead of `/settings/webhooks`, so the whole alert setup (register a webhook → create a rule → see its firing history) stays in one module without a jump out. The reused page is **parameterized by context** so its copy is honest in each surface (Rule 3): Settings keeps the deploy framing + the "delivery coming soon" notice; the observability surface omits that notice (alert delivery is live today — M84) and offers the `alert.threshold_breached` event — no misleading deploy copy under the alert-notification surface.
- **Agents empty-state copy names the structural signal (theo-lens M85).** With the engine's Agents derivation broadened to include structural Claude-Code agents (`claude_code.*` spans without an explicit `agent.name`), the Agents page "No agents yet" copy is updated to say an agent appears via `agent.name` **or** a structural `claude_code.*` span — so the empty state matches the new derivation and no longer implies `agent.name` is the only path.
- **Fired-alert history — an alert firing is auditable in-app, not just "last fired" (theo-lens M84).** The alert evaluator now records ONE `lens_fired_alerts` row per breach (the metric value at fire time, comparator, threshold, when) in the SAME transaction as the webhook enqueue + the status flip — so a breach atomically yields exactly one delivery AND one history row (a failed history INSERT rolls the whole fire back, retried next tick; anti-storm dedup on `(rule_id, transition_id)`). A new paginated read `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/alert-rules/{id}/history` returns the firings newest-first, gated on the rule's owner (a foreign/non-existent rule → 404, never a leak). The Alerts page gains a per-rule **History** expander rendering the firing timeline (value vs threshold, when) with an honest "never fired" empty and a fail-closed typed error (a load failure never reads as "never fired"). Where the rule carried only a single `last_triggered_at`, the firing history is now durable + queryable.
- **Copilot answer feedback — thumbs up/down restored + persisted (theo-lens M83).** The Ask-copilot thread's thumbs control (removed in M72 as decorative theatre) is back and now **wired to a real endpoint**: a thumbs up/down on an answer POSTs `{turnId,label}` to `POST /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}/ask/feedback` (new BFF proxy, fail-closed per-tenant guard, write-scope). The pressed thumb is optimistic (immediate); a failed POST **fails closed to a typed "Feedback not saved" alert and reverts the local state** — no fabricated success. Clicking the active thumb clears the vote — the clear now **persists** via `DELETE …/ask/feedback?turnId=` (so the shown thumb never diverges from the stored vote; a failed clear reverts + shows the typed error); a re-vote toggles up↔down (persisted as an upstream UPSERT). Feedback is independent of the LLM provider, so it works even when the copilot itself is down.
- **Dashboard: rename & delete datasets and evaluators (theo-lens M82).** The Datasets and Evaluators lists gain inline **Rename** (edit-in-place, Enter to commit / Escape to cancel) and **Delete** (a confirm dialog; deleting a dataset cascades its items + experiment runs upstream) — previously both surfaces were create-only with no way to fix a typo'd name or remove a stale resource. A rename to a name already in use surfaces a precise **"name already exists"** message (the forwarded `409`), never a generic failure. Reuses the M75 list-management pattern (inline-rename Input + `ConfirmDialog`). Backed by the BFF proxies below.
- **BFF: dataset & evaluator rename/delete proxies + upstream-status propagation (theo-lens M82).** Three new thin proxies on the lens dashboard BFF — `PUT /v1/dashboard/lens/datasets/{id}` (rename), `DELETE /v1/dashboard/lens/datasets/{id}` (delete, cascades runs+items in theo-lens), `PUT /v1/dashboard/lens/evaluators/{id}` (rename/partial-edit) — each behind the fail-closed per-tenant guard, forwarding the browser body verbatim to theo-lens's real native routes. The BFF now **forwards theo-lens's real non-2xx status** (a rename's `409` name-conflict, `404` absent, `400` invalid) with its `{code,message}` body instead of flattening every upstream failure to `502` — the browser can show "name already taken" precisely. A transport/resolver error (no HTTP status) still collapses to `502` (fail-closed). Backs the M82 dashboard rename/delete UI.
- **Dashboard: the Traces and Sessions lists are now server-paginated (theo-lens M81).** Both reads return a workspace-scoped `total` (via the labeling-queue two-query technique) and the applied `limit`/`offset`, and the dashboard renders a pager (Prev/Next + "Showing X–Y of N") reaching page 2+ — large tenants are navigable instead of silently capped (traces) or unbounded (sessions). Sessions previously dropped the pagination params the BFF already forwarded; they are now honored. Reuses the existing `limit`/`offset` plumbing end-to-end (no BFF change) and the M70 pager. `count(*)` is scoped by `workspace_id` (isolation preserved).
