## [0.27.0] - 2026-07-08

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).

- **Dashboard: the Ask copilot's span citations are now clickable (theo-lens M80).** A copilot answer that cites a span (`[span:id]` → an inline badge) is now a **link to that span in the trace peek** — clicking it opens `/observability/traces?trace=…&spanId=…`, which opens the URL-addressable peek (M76) and selects + scrolls to the cited span (a stale/unknown span id falls back to the root — never a broken link, and an unresolvable citation stays plain text). The grounded conversation is now navigable, not just readable. Client-side wiring reusing react-router `Link` + the existing `?spanId=` selection (no copilot-schema or BFF change).
- **Dashboard: evaluator scores are now visible where you look at data (theo-lens M78).** A span's **machine evaluations** (name + score/label + explanation, from the ingest-populated `span_evaluations` carried on the trace-detail read) now render as a distinct **"Evaluations" section** in the span detail — separate from the human tags/notes, so a machine verdict is never mistaken for a human label. The traces list gains an **"Evals" count column** (from the list read's `evaluationsCount`, an O(1) subquery already on the wire) so scored traces are findable without opening each one. Both were already in the fetched payloads — the dashboard's types just omitted them; an unscored span/trace shows an honest em-dash, never a fabricated 0. Pure/unit-tested; reuses `@usetheo/ui` `Badge` + `formatScore` (no BFF change).
- **Dashboard: guards now show what they caught (theo-lens M77).** Each guard row gains a **"View breaches"** action that opens a panel listing the traces that failed the gate — read from the existing-but-unused `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/guards/{id}/breaches` — each **deep-linking to its trace peek** (`?trace=`). The panel frames the list with the guard's own rule ("Traces failing {evaluator} {operator} {threshold}") and an honest empty state ("no trace has failed this guard yet"); a load error fails closed to a typed Alert. The breaches read is flag-only (`{traceIds}`) by theo-lens design — the panel surfaces the breaching traces without fabricating a per-breach score/time. Reuses `@usetheo/ui` `Sheet`/`Link` (no BFF change).

### Fixed

- **Dashboard: the Alerts "register a webhook first" prerequisite is no longer a dead-end (theo-lens M79).** The notice shown when a workspace has no webhook (the alert delivery target) previously said "Add one under Settings › Webhooks" as plain text with no link; it now links directly to the **`/settings/webhooks`** page — one click from the fix, matching the v5 one-clear-action convention. The create form stays correctly gated until a webhook exists. Reuses react-router `Link` (no BFF change).

## [0.26.0] - 2026-07-08

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).

- **Dashboard: every observability sidebar entry is self-serve — no more dead-ends (theo-lens M73).** The **Experiments** landing (entered without `?datasetId=`) now offers a **dataset `<Select>`** (fed by the existing `GET /datasets`) that seeds a run right there — a run is no longer only startable via `experiments?datasetId=` from Traces (zero datasets keeps the honest "create a regression dataset" text, no fabricated picker). The **Ask** empty state gains a **"Browse traces →"** CTA, and the **Datasets** + **Guards** "create your first X" empty states gain a CTA that focuses their create input — so the three divergent empty-state patterns collapse to one convention (every empty state has exactly one clear next action). Reuses `@usetheo/ui` `Select`/`EmptyState` + existing reads (no new dependency, no BFF change). RED-first tests pin each actionable control (the picker seeds `?datasetId=`, the CTA links to traces, the focus-CTAs focus the create input).
- **Dashboard: the LLM-judge evaluator class is now reachable from the UI + the labeling queue links out to traces (theo-lens M76).** The Evaluators create form gains a **kind `<Select>` (Rule / LLM judge)** that swaps the config fields — a Rule collects a `predicate` (with a concrete example hint), an LLM judge collects a `prompt` + `model` — and POSTs the matching theo-lens discriminated-union body; the whole `llm_judge` class was previously uncreatable. The **labeling-queue** Trace id is now a **link to its source trace** (the trace peek is now URL-addressable via `?trace=` on the Traces page — deep-linkable/shareable), and **"Export as dataset" is guarded** until at least one item is resolved (a warning + disabled confirm instead of a silent empty-dataset export). Reuses `@usetheo/ui` `Select`/`Link` (no new dependency, no BFF change).
- **Dashboard: custom dashboards are now distinguishable and manageable (theo-lens M75).** Creating boards from the template no longer produces three indistinguishable "Tracing Overview" rows — "New from template" now picks a **unique default name** ("Tracing Overview", "Tracing Overview 2", …, filling the lowest free suffix), the list shows a **Created** column, and each row has an inline **Rename** (Enter commits, Escape cancels — the PUT echoes the board's config back so widgets are never blanked) and a **Delete** (ConfirmDialog → existing `DELETE` verb). Reuses `@usetheo/ui` `ConfirmDialog`/`Timestamp`/`Input` + existing cloud verbs (no BFF change). The unique-name helper is a pure, unit-tested function. Datasets/Evaluators lack the server-side rename/delete verbs for the same treatment — tracked in theo-lens#43.

### Changed

- **Dashboard: the Overview stops over-dramatizing tiny samples (theo-lens M76).** The KPI period-over-period **delta is now suppressed on a small-N window** (fewer than 6 buckets, a documented constant) — a bold ±% arrow over 3–4 buckets is noise, so the tile shows an honest "window too sparse" hint instead (the M68 em-dash-over-undefined honesty policy, extended to small samples). The **trend chart marks each point on a sparse series (< 5 points)** so a 2–4 point line no longer reads as a cliff. Both are pure, unit-tested changes; the delta math itself is unchanged (the suppression is a presentation decision).
- **Dashboard: the Agents and Sessions empty states now explain WHY they are empty (theo-lens M74).** Both derived catalogs previously read a generic "No X yet" that looked like the product had lost the user's data. **Agents** now names the actual prerequisite — an agent appears once its spans carry the `agent.name` attribute (the OpenTelemetry `agent.*` convention) — with an "Instrument an agent →" docs CTA, mirroring the Sessions pattern. **Sessions** now names its grouping key — sessions group traces that share a `session.id` attribute. Copy-only (no new fetch, no dependency, no BFF change); RED-first tests assert each empty state names its prerequisite. The deeper question of whether the server-side agent derivation should recognize structural subagents (Claude Code) beyond `agent.name` is tracked in theo-lens#42.

### Fixed

- **Dashboard: a 204 No Content response (e.g. a successful DELETE) is no longer mistaken for a failure (theo-lens M75).** `cloudFetch` called `resp.json()` on the empty body of a 204, which threw and was re-thrown as a network error under `throwOnError` — so a *successful* dashboard delete surfaced "Could not delete". `cloudFetch` now returns `null` for 204/205 No Content. Caught by a live dogfood (the server returned 204 while the UI showed an error); RED-first regression test added. Pre-existing bug that only surfaced via the first `throwOnError` DELETE caller.
- **Dashboard: the Evaluators delete button announces the evaluator name, not a raw id (theo-lens M75, a11y).** The delete action's accessible name was `Delete e92e0cf9-…` (the raw id) — unusable to a screen-reader user; it now reads `Delete evaluator {name}`, matching the Run button. RED-first test asserts the name-based label and the absence of any id-based label.
- **Dashboard: the trace-detail peek panel is no longer cramped (theo-lens).** The row-click trace-detail

## [0.25.0] - 2026-07-08

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Dashboard: Agent Graph — agentic control-flow visualization on the trace detail (theo-lens M67).** Closes the agent-flow-viz gap (Arize screen 9, the last v3 milestone): a new **Graph** tab on the trace detail (beside Tree ⇄ Transcript) renders the run's spans as a **deterministic pure-SVG node-edge graph** — nodes carry a **semantic kind** (`llm`/`tool`/`retriever`/`embedding`/`chain`/`unknown`) derived client-side from the span's own signals (a faithful port of theo-lens `span-kind.ts`; the DTO `.kind` is the raw OTLP numeric), edges follow **parent/call order** (`parentSpanId`), and the **root→selected execution path** is highlighted. A node click selects the span via the M58 `onSelect(spanId)` contract (synced to the URL `?spanId=` + the tree/timeline); an oversize trace (> `GRAPH_NODE_CAP`) shows an honest **"trace too large to graph (N spans)"** state instead of the canvas. The layout is a **pure, deterministic, unit-tested helper** (`buildTraceGraph` — depth-then-order, edges from `parentSpanId` with **cycle/self-loop + missing-parent guards**: a self-loop is broken by a visited set, a missing-parent span is attached to the root, never dropped). **No graph-viz library** — the installed `react-force-graph-2d` is a non-deterministic physics canvas, wrong for a precise path-highlighted DAG (ADR D1); **no new runtime dependency** (`/deps-audit` no-op). The `<svg>` carries `role="img"` + an `aria-label` + an **sr-only node summary** (the a11y differentiator no peer ships). **Dashboard-only, no theo-lens change** (the span tree + kind signals are already in the trace-detail DTO). Composed from `@usetheo/ui` chrome + `lucide-react`. Tests: **9 pure layout unit** (deterministic placement, kind precedence, root→selected onPath, default root→deepest-leaf, cycle/self-loop guard, missing-parent attach-not-drop, over-cap truncated, single-span) + **6 component** (graph build from a real fixture, node-select URL sync, deeplink selected+path highlight, oversize honest state, malformed-parent negative, reachable via toggle). Full `trace-detail` suite (86) + lens+layout suite (295) green; `tsc`/`eslint` clean; `vite build` ships the graph in the trace-detail chunk; all files ≤500 LoC. Live-verified on a real multi-agent trace (`claude_code.interaction → llm(opus) + subagent → [tool.grep, llm(haiku)]`) through the real Model-B edge — kinds derived live, node-click set `?spanId=`, and the execution path re-highlighted to root→subagent→tool. Plan `theo-lens/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m67-agent-graph-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard + BFF: Custom Dashboards — widget board + Tracing Overview template (theo-lens M66).** Closes the custom-dashboard gap (Arize screen 8): a new **Dashboards** page (`/observability/dashboards`, Observe nav group) where a workspace assembles a board from a **fixed widget palette** — **KPI tile / trend chart / top-N table** — each bound to an existing M51/M60 observability series; a code-defined **"Tracing Overview" template** (KPI strip + a traffic trend + top models by cost) instantiates in one click when no board exists. Widgets are edited as an **ordered list** (add / remove / move-up / move-down — no drag-grid, ADR D4: the peers all use `react-grid-layout` and pay a maintenance cost M66 declines; no new runtime dependency). Storage is a per-**workspace** `lens_dashboards` table (engine migration `000055`) behind a new Go `LensDashboardsHandler` CRUD (`POST`/`GET`(list)/`GET{id}`/`PUT{id}`/`DELETE{id}` under `/v1/dashboard/lens/dashboards`, `requireJWT`) scoped by `tenant_id` from the Principal (a shared team board — D5; `created_by` audit-only); the `config` JSONB is **validated at the write boundary** (fixed widget-type/metric palette, ≤24 widgets, title cap → typed `400`). Cross-tenant get/update/delete collapse to **404-not-403** (existence-leak prevention — the M52 saved-views pattern). Every widget derives from **ONE shared `/observability` + ONE `/cost` fetch** (the N-widget fan-out risk avoided by reuse, not new infra). **Honesty (never a fabricated number):** absent data (fetch failed / not loaded) → an em-dash `—`; a **rate/percentile over an EMPTY series** (`error_rate`, `p95_latency` for a quiet tenant) is *undefined* → also `—`, NOT a misleading `0%`/`0ms`; a **count/sum** (`traces`, `cost`, `tokens_by_model`) over an empty range is a defined measurement → an honest `0`/`$0.0000`. A load/scope error surfaces a typed `Alert` (`description=`), never a fabricated board. KPI tiles reuse `@usetheo/ui` `MetricCard`; charts reuse `MetricTrendChart` (`role="img"` + `aria-label` + an sr-only summary table — the a11y differentiator no peer ships); top-N uses `DataTable`. Composed from `@usetheo/ui` (`MetricCard`/`DataTable`/`Select`/`EmptyState`/`Card`/`PageShell`) + M57 formatters — no new dependency. Tests: 14 Go handler sqlmock (auth-401, empty-name/empty-widgets/unknown-type/unknown-metric/oversize → 400, insert `ldb-` id, list tenant-scoped, get/update/delete cross-tenant → 404, malformed-id 400) + 8 pure widget-model unit (template validity, validation parity, series binding, absent→em-dash + empty-series-rate/percentile→em-dash, count/sum→honest-0, ordered-list ops) + 9 dashboard component (list/template CTA, start-from-template creates a board, board renders from the single shared fetch, add/remove/reorder PUT round-trip, load-error/scope-deny typed Alert, widget-series-error + quiet-tenant-empty show em-dash-not-fabricated, chart aria-label). Dashboard lens + layout suites green (280 tests); `go build`/`vet`/`test` green; `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all files ≤500 LoC. No theo-lens change (the board data reads the existing observability BFF) → cross-tenant isolation is proven at the Go handler (404-not-403, tenant-scoped), the M52/M53 precedent. Plan `theo-lens/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m66-custom-dashboards-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard + BFF: Experiments — regression-dataset-run + run-to-run compare (theo-lens M64).** Closes the experiments gap (Arize screen 3): a **"Create regression dataset"** action in the traces bulk-selection toolbar turns the selected traces into a per-tenant dataset (reuses the M44 dataset routes — no new dataset code), then routes to a new **Experiments** page (`/observability/experiments`, Evaluate nav group). There, a run form runs the dataset × a **variant** (model / system-prompt override) through the **M33 provider path** server-side, rendering per-row **output + evaluator scores** (`DataTable`) + an aggregate **pass-rate** / avg-score / total-cost summary; a **compare view** (`?compare=id,id`) shows two runs side-by-side with a per-run summary + a **pass-count delta**, and a **"View trace"** on any row opens the source span detail (M58 `Sheet`). Every write goes through the Go BFF as a fail-closed verbatim passthrough (401 without a tenant) to theo-lens's per-verb-scoped routes (the CWE-441 second half — `observability:write` for the run, `observability:read` for list/get/compare). Fail-closed: a `503` (no provider) / `429` (rate-limited) / `403` / `404` surfaces a typed `Alert`, never fabricated rows; a per-item provider error is shown as an error row (never a fabricated output). Composed from `@usetheo/ui` (`DataTable`/`Card`/`Input`/`PageShell`) + M57 formatters + M58 Sheet — no new dependency. Component tests (run→per-row scores + aggregate, runs list, compare deltas, View-Trace, provider-error typed Alert, create-regression-dataset from selection). Dashboard lens + layout suites green (308 tests); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all files ≤500 LoC. Backed by the theo-lens experiment-runs store + the DIP run engine + the hermetic ForwardAuth cross-tenant e2e (theo-data invariant 6). Plan `theo-lens/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m64-experiments-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard + BFF: Labeling Queues — the human-in-the-loop review workbench (theo-lens M63 — first v3 milestone).** Closes the annotation gap (Arize screen 6): a per-span **"Add to labeling queue"** entry-point in the trace-detail span panel (a single affordance, not a duplicate of the M44 tags/annotations flow) enqueues a span for review; a new **Queues** page (`/observability/labeling-queue`, under the Evaluate nav group) lists the workspace's queue items in a `DataTable` and opens a resolve `Sheet` (span context + a **label / score / note** form with real `<Label>`s) that writes the resolution back scoped to the Principal; an **"Export as dataset"** action turns the resolved items into a **golden dataset** (reuses the M44 dataset store). Every write goes through the Go BFF as a fail-closed verbatim passthrough (401 without a tenant; body forwarded unchanged) to theo-lens's per-verb-scoped routes (the CWE-441 second half — `observability:read` for list/get, `observability:write` for enqueue/resolve/export). Fail-closed: a `403` scope-deny / `404` cross-tenant / `400` validation error surfaces a typed `Alert`, never a fabricated success. Composed from `@usetheo/ui` (`DataTable`/`Sheet`/`Label`/`Input`/`Textarea`/`PageShell`) — no new dependency. Component tests (queue list, empty state, resolve round-trip with the scoped write body, export→dataset, scope-deny typed error, the single enqueue entry-point posting exactly once). Dashboard lens + layout suites green (302 tests); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all files ≤500 LoC. Backed by the theo-lens store + routes + the hermetic ForwardAuth cross-tenant e2e (theo-data invariant 6). Plan `theo-lens/.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m63-labeling-queues-plan.md`.


### Fixed
- **Dashboard: observability craft & completeness polish (theo-lens M72).** The **trace detail** gains a headline **summary strip** (total duration / ∑ cost / ∑ tokens / error-count / distinct models) and **SpanDetail** gains a **Cost** stat + renders each **event's time and attributes** (an exception event's stacktrace was previously invisible). A `formatScore` helper lands in `lib/format.ts` and the experiments/agents raw `.toFixed()`/count bypasses route through it + `formatNumber` (consistent + em-dash on absent, never a fabricated `0.00`). **Write-actions no longer fail silently:** the Alerts create/toggle/delete and the playground save-as-dataset surface a typed write-error `Alert` (`throwOnError` + `retry:false`). The Ask copilot's **decorative thumbs feedback was removed** (it only mutated local state, never persisted — filed as usetheodev/theo-cloud#181) and the thread now **auto-scrolls** to the latest turn (and its error Alert now renders its body — a latent #175 in `components/lens`). The PII **Reveal** control is now shown **only to an authorized viewer** — an unauthorized viewer sees a quiet "masked" hint instead of a permanently-disabled inert button (fail-closed, no theatre). RED-first tests pin the summary, formatScore, both write-error surfaces, the PII hide, and the event attributes.
- **Dashboard: accessibility baseline on the trace detail — real ARIA tree + operable timeline (theo-lens M71).** The span **tree** is now a real ARIA tree: `role="tree"` on the container + `role="treeitem"` with `aria-level` / `aria-posinset` / `aria-setsize` / `aria-expanded` / `aria-selected` on every row (was a plain nested `<div>` a screen reader could not announce as a tree). Each **waterfall** timeline row now carries a distinct, informative `aria-label` (`Timeline: <span> · <duration>`) instead of one shared "Select this span in the timeline" for every row, so the timeline is operable by a screen reader; the tree/graph test queries are scoped to their lane (the fix the audit called for). The **agent graph** `<svg>` is now `role="group"` (was `role="img"`, which hid its interactive span nodes from assistive tech) and its raw-hex palette is tokenized to theme utilities (`stroke-primary`/`fill-card`/`fill-muted-foreground`/…) so it stays legible + contrast-safe in light and dark. The **Ask copilot** thread is a `role="log"` `aria-live="polite"` region so new answers are announced, and the **Alerts** enable toggle carries `aria-pressed`. Every lens text input already resolves to a `<Label htmlFor>` or an `aria-label` (verified by a sweep — no placeholder-only inputs remain). RED-first a11y assertions pin the tree roles, the distinct timeline labels, the graph group role, the copilot log region, and the toggle pressed-state.
- **Dashboard: the Observability navigation + eval loop are wired end-to-end (theo-lens M70).** Cross-screen jumps close the dead-ends: a **Sessions** row jumps to the traces list pre-filtered by session (`traces?session=`), a **Dataset** row jumps to the Experiments run form seeded with it (`experiments?datasetId=`), a **dataset item** opens its source-trace span peek, and the **Experiments** runs list gained a compare-picker (tick two runs → `?compare=`). The sidebar nav and the Overview "Explore" grid are now generated from ONE `LENS_CAPABILITIES` registry (they can no longer drift — the audit found three disagreeing maps), **Alerts** is added to the sidebar, and the Explore cards use SPA `<Link>` nav instead of a full-page reload. The **Labeling queue** gained a real Prev/Next pager + "Showing X–Y of N" (page 2+ was previously unreachable); the **Traces** list shows an honest loaded-row count (the list BFF returns no total — filed as usetheodev/theo-cloud#180). RED-first tests pin every jump + the registry-no-drift invariant + the pager.
- **Dashboard: Guards create is usable — evaluator + operator are now `<Select>`s, not a free-text id (theo-lens M70, usetheodev/theo-cloud#179).** The Guards create form demanded a raw evaluator id that the Evaluators screen never surfaced, and hard-coded the comparator to `gte` — making the flow effectively unusable. The evaluator is now chosen from a `<Select>` fed by `GET /evaluators` (value = id, label = name), the operator is a `<Select>` (`≥/>/≤/</= threshold` + `matches label`), and the threshold field is hidden AND omitted from the request for a `label_eq` guard (no fabricated threshold). RED-first tests pin the select-driven create, the label_eq threshold-omission, and the delete flow.
- **Dashboard: observability error Alerts now render their body — the #175 "system killer" is closed (theo-lens M69, usetheodev/theo-cloud#175).** The `@usetheo/ui` `Alert` renders only `title`/`description` and silently drops JSX `children`, so ~17 lens error/notice sites shipped title-only — the failing trace id (`compare.tsx`), the "register a webhook" instruction (`alerts.tsx`), and the typed `errorMessage(error)` detail (`labeling-queue.tsx`) were all invisible AND unannounced. Introduced an app-side `Alert` guard (`src/components/ui/alert-guard.tsx`) that renders `children` as the body when no explicit `description` is given (an explicit `description` still wins), and pointed all 16 lens pages at it — so every already-written error body now renders and is screen-reader-announced. A **recurrence guard** test (`src/lib/lens-alert-guard.test.ts`) fails if any lens page reaches for the raw `@usetheo/ui` Alert again. Body-text tests pin the worst-loss sites (compare trace id, labeling-queue typed message, alerts webhook instruction). The list **loading skeletons** were also made honest: a shared table-shaped `ListSkeleton` (`role="status"` + `aria-label="Loading …"`) replaces the 8 bare unlabeled `<Skeleton/>` sites (sessions/traces/agents/evaluators/guards/alerts/datasets), matching the `dashboards.tsx` exemplar. Full lens + components suites green (727); `tsc`/`eslint` clean. (Upstream `Omit<…,"children">` hardening of the primitive is filed separately.)
- **Dashboard: trace-detail Tree/Waterfall/Transcript/Compare no longer flag OK spans as ERROR (theo-lens M68, usetheodev/theo-cloud#176).** The span error-predicate was `statusCode !== "0"` in four views, which paints an OK span (`"1"` / `STATUS_CODE_OK`) red — live-reproduced in the M67 dogfood (4 OK + 1 ERROR span rendered 5× "ERROR"). Introduced a single canonical `isSpanError` (`src/lib/span-status.ts`) over the sanctioned OTLP error-token set `{"2","ERROR","STATUS_CODE_ERROR"}` (unknown/absent → not an error, fail-safe) and wired the Tree, Waterfall, Transcript, Compare, and Graph views to it — retiring all four divergent copies (three wrong `!== "0"`, one correct-but-duplicated in graph-layout). RED-first `span-status.test.ts` + a strengthened `compare-core.test.ts` pin that `"1"`/`STATUS_CODE_OK` are NOT errors; the `trace-detail` suite (86) stays green.
- **Dashboard: custom-dashboard "Tokens by model" trend now plots tokens, not cost (theo-lens M68, usetheodev/theo-cloud#178).** `bindTrend` routed both the `cost` and `tokens_by_model` widget metrics through `costByModelSeries` (which sums `costUsd`), so a "Tokens by model" widget plotted dollars. Added a sibling pure `tokensByModelSeries` (summing `totalTokens`, ranked top-N by tokens) — both now delegate to one shared `byModelSeries(rows, valueOf, topN)` pivot so they never diverge again. RED-first `lens-series.test.ts` + `widgets.test.ts` pin that the tokens trend ranks/plots by tokens (gpt-4o-mini leads by 5000 tokens) while the cost trend still ranks by dollars (gpt-4o leads).
- **Dashboard: observability data-honesty sweep — stop showing misleading values (theo-lens M68).** Five verified paths where a value misrepresented reality, all fixed with RED-first tests: (1) **Overview KPI strip** computed `error_rate = 0%` and `p95 = 0ms` for a quiet tenant — a rate/percentile over an empty window is *undefined*, now an em-dash (traces/cost stay honest `0`/`$0.0000`); (2) **Experiments per-row score** rendered `0.00` and a fabricated pass color for an un-evaluated row (usetheodev/theo-cloud#177) — now an em-dash, with the accent claimed only when the evaluator itself labelled the row "pass" (no fabricated `0.5` threshold); (3) **Alerts status badge** painted a green "success" for any non-`TRIGGERED` rule including disabled/unknown — now an explicit `alertStatusVariant(status, enabled)` map (disabled → neutral "Disabled", `OK` → success, `TRIGGERED` → destructive, unknown → neutral); (4) **Datasets items panel** rendered a failed detail load as "No items" (error-as-empty) — the detail query now `throwOnError` + a typed `Alert` branch; (5) **absent counts** (`itemCount`, `traceCount`) rendered a fabricated `0` — now an em-dash when absent, a real `0` preserved. Both the custom-dashboard AND the Overview **cost KPI** were single-sourced from the same range-scoped observability fetch as the cost trend (a new pure `costByPeriod` sums cost per period from `tokensByModel`), removing a redundant all-time `/cost` fetch that made each KPI disagree with its trend's window; the custom-dashboard **error-rate KPI** now also em-dashes a rate over a present-but-quiet window (traces `0`), not just a fully empty one. The traces-list **status cell** now flags an OTLP error via the shared `isSpanError` (the previous `=== 'error'` string check silently showed a `statusCode` `2`/`STATUS_CODE_ERROR` trace as non-error — a #176-family false-negative that HID errors in the primary list), and the Agents **trace count** em-dashes an absent value. Full lens + lib suites green; `tsc`/`eslint`/`vite build` clean.
- **Dashboard: Experiments compare view now shows the aggregate pass-rate + per-row score deltas (theo-lens M64 review).** The compare view consumes the new `deltas` from `GET …/experiment-runs/compare`: an aggregate **pass-rate** / avg-score / cost delta (replacing the previous naive pass-*count*-of-first-two) plus a per-row **Δ vs A** score-delta column aligned by source trace — delivering the DoD3 "per-row score deltas + aggregate pass-rate delta" in full. Component test asserts the pass-rate delta value + the per-row delta column.
- **Dashboard: Experiments error/empty Alerts now render their body (theo-lens M64 review).** The `@usetheo/ui` `Alert` renders its body from the `description` prop, not `children`; the experiments page passed the typed error message (503/429/403/404) + empty-state text as children, so only the title showed. Switched to `description=` so the actionable message is visible; a new 403 scope-deny test asserts the permission body renders. The same latent misuse in 6 other lens pages is tracked in usetheodev/theo-cloud#175 (out of M64 scope).

## [0.24.0] - 2026-07-07

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Dashboard: playground rebuilt as a side-by-side variant comparison + View-Trace (theo-lens M62 — the last SOTA-experience v2 milestone).** The M55 single-model "Original vs Replay" panel becomes the SOTA default (Langfuse n-window / Opik 6 / Phoenix ≤4): a horizontal scroll row of **N editable variant columns** (up to 4) next to the **read-only original baseline**, each re-run against the real provider server-side (the M55 path, no BFF change). Per-variant: editable role messages + model override + an optional **structured-output JSON schema** (validated client-side — invalid → Run disabled; forwarded to the provider as `response_format` json_schema). **Run-all** fans out one replay per variant (bounded by the variant cap + the server per-workspace rate-limit — no client retry loop, `retry:false`); each output `Card` shows `{provider·model, latency, tokens, cost}` via the M57 formatters — **cost is the real provider `usage.cost`** surfaced additively (theo-lens M62 P0) or an honest **em-dash** when absent (never `$0`/fabricated). An output **greys when its inputs changed since the last run** (stale, pure `variant-model.ts` staleness). A **View Trace** action opens the original span's detail in a `Sheet` (reuses M58 — the playground stays mounted). Fail-closed: a provider 502/503/429/404 surfaces a typed `Alert` on that column, never a fabricated output. The replay is ephemeral (M44 read-only — the trace is never mutated); the only persist path is the per-variant opt-in **save-as-dataset**. All output is plain text (XSS-safe — no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`). Composed from `@usetheo/ui` + M57 + M58 — **no new dependency, no BFF/Go change** (BFF is a verbatim passthrough). Pure `variant-model.ts` unit-tested (staleness / deep-copy / runKey); `variant-column`/`playground` component tests (multi-variant edit→run→compare, per-variant metrics, cost em-dash, no-mutation-of-original, View-Trace open, provider-error, cross-tenant refusal, XSS-safe, invalid-schema-blocks-run). Full lens suite green (261 tests); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; every touched file ≤500 LoC. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m62-playground-side-by-side-plan.md`.

## [0.23.0] - 2026-07-07

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **Dashboard: Ask copilot rebuilt as a persistent conversation thread (theo-lens M61).** The single-turn Ask stub (a manual trace-id `Input` → one answer) becomes a **multi-turn thread docked to the open trace** (Opik `TraceAIViewer` model): `/observability/ask` reads the trace from the URL (`?traceId=`, carried by a per-row **"Ask copilot"** affordance on the traces list — **no manual UUID entry**), fetches the trace tree (reused M58 read — Rule 9) for a `spanId → name` entity map, and renders `<AskThread>`. The thread is **client-side over the single-turn M33 provider path** (no BFF change): each turn is one `POST /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}/ask` with `retry:false` (never a billable retry loop); it is **keyed by `traceId` and cleared on change** so one trace's conversation never bleeds into another; assistant answers render through the reused **XSS-safe `Markdown` wrapper** (react-markdown + rehype-sanitize) with `[span:<id>]` markers resolved to linked span names (an unknown id stays plain text — never fabricated); role-labeled `Card` turns, 4 canned **starter prompts**, per-answer **thumbs feedback**, a per-turn span-grounding citation `Badge`, and a ⌘↵ composer. Fail-closed: 502/503/429/404 → a **typed `Alert`, no fabricated answer** (the cross-tenant-404 refusal is tested). Pure `ask-thread-model.ts` (entity map + citation resolver + starter prompts) unit-tested. Full lens suite green (248 tests); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all touched files ≤500 LoC. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m61-ask-copilot-conversation-thread-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard: observability Overview analytics — KPI deltas + one shared click-to-expand trend (theo-lens M60).** The four `StatTile`s become `@usetheo/ui` **`MetricCard`** tiles (Traces / Error rate / Latency p95 / Cost) each carrying a **period-over-period delta** (↑/↓ + %) with `invertTrend` on the "up is bad" metrics (cost/latency/error-rate) and an honest **"N/A"** when the window is too sparse to split. The delta is a pure, unit-tested `periodDelta` (`src/components/lens/overview-metrics.ts`) computed **half-vs-half of the already-fetched M51 window** — no BFF change, division-by-zero/un-splittable → `na` (never `Infinity`/`NaN`). A `ResizeObserver` drives **width-adaptive degradation** (`cardMode`: full → no-delta → icon-only). The four always-shown trend charts collapse into **ONE shared chart** that swaps metric on a KPI-tile click (URL-persisted `?metric=`), reusing the existing **pure-SVG `MetricTrendChart`** (its a11y `role=img` + visually-hidden value table + empty-state come for free) + the M51 range `Select`. **Charting-lib audit verdict: reuse the pure-SVG chart — NO new dependency added** (M51 ADR D1 + parsimony rung-4; the blueprint's recharts suggestion is overridden by theo-lens's fixed-metric-set precedent). Full lens suite green (35 component tests); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all touched files ≤500 LoC. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m60-overview-analytics-kpi-delta-trend-plan.md`.

Data-only change — every route/path is unchanged; the workspace switcher is untouched. `app-sidebar.test.tsx` asserts the three group titles + all items at their paths. Full sidebar + lens suites green (229 passed); `tsc`/`eslint` clean. (Note: `app-sidebar.tsx` is 594 LoC — over the 500-LoC guide, but it was already 583 pre-M59; the +11-line data regrouping did not introduce the overage, and extracting the whole menu registry is deferred as separate pre-existing debt.) Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m59-traces-list-density-chips-nav-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard: traces-list saved-views polish — columns capture + naming Dialog + shareable viewId (theo-lens M59 Phase 3).** A **column-visibility** control (`@usetheo/ui` `Checkbox` panel) toggles the optional spine columns (latency/tokens/cost), encoded into a single **versioned `cols=` URL param** by a pure, round-trip-safe `traces/view-encoding.ts` (`encodeColumns`/`decodeColumns` — an unknown/legacy/malformed payload **warn-and-migrates** to defaults, never throws; 6 unit tests) — so the existing M52 saved view captures **columns + density** with **no schema change** (ADR D4). The inline save form becomes a **naming `Dialog`**; applying a view marks it active and appends a **`?viewId=`** handle (shareable by URL — a bare `?viewId=` link re-applies the stored view once, guarded against a re-apply loop). Per-tenant isolation preserved: `viewId` only ever resolves against the Principal-scoped saved-views list (a cross-tenant test asserts another tenant's view never appears). Full lens suite green (198 passed); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; all M59 files ≤500 LoC. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m59-traces-list-density-chips-nav-plan.md`.

The chip⇄URL mapping is a pure, round-trip-safe `traces/filter-model.ts` (`filtersToChips`/`removeChip`/`chipToParam`/`paramsToFilters` — round-trip identity is unit-proven, 7 tests) that writes the **SAME** `nuqs` param keys, so `buildTraceQuery` + the M49 BFF whitelist are **unchanged** (no BFF change). The primary search box stays (debounced). Composed from `@usetheo/ui` `Select`/`Input`/`Badge` — no `Popover` (not exported), no new dependency. A **StatusDot** joins the status Badge; the raw compare checkbox is replaced by the `@usetheo/ui` `Checkbox`. No new dependency, no BFF change — reuses `@usetheo/ui` + M57's `format.ts`. `traces.tsx` further decomposed (`traces/{columns,table-toolbar,peek-sheet}.tsx`, every file ≤500 LoC). Full lens suite green (179 passed); `tsc`/`eslint` clean. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/m59-traces-list-density-chips-nav-plan.md`.
- **Dashboard: trace-detail reader-mode (theo-lens M58 — the SOTA-experience v2 flagship).** Rebuilt the observability trace-detail into a reader-first surface, reuse-first on `@usetheo/ui` (one new audited dep: `react-markdown@10` + `rehype-sanitize@6`, OSV-clean, single import site). (DoD1) a **Tree ⇄ Transcript toggle** (URL `?view=`) rendering a virtualized reader-mode feed of role-labeled Card rows (StatusDot + span name + inline output preview + micro-stats) with **collapsible subagent-group headers**; (DoD2) waterfall polish — a **nice-interval time axis** (≥70px ticks, ms/s/min), a hover-needle with an absolute-time Tooltip, error=destructive, selected=ring, and a latent `∑` aggregated-parent-cost badge (renders once per-span cost is on the wire); (DoD3) **role-labeled IO cards** (not bubbles) with sanitized markdown (`rehype-sanitize`, no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`), a raw-JSON toggle, dedicated tool-call cards linked by call-id, a redacted/thinking block, and long-history "Show N more"; (DoD4) **namespace-grouped, collapsible attribute sections** (`gen_ai`/`llm`/`http`/…) + a promoted-badge header (model/tokens/cost/level) + per-value copy + **display-time PII masking** with a **fail-closed** scope-gated Reveal (the raw value is provably absent from the pre-reveal DOM — a superiority surface no SOTA peer ships); (DoD5) a detail **breadcrumb** + deep-link `?spanId=&tab=&view=` + keyboard `]`/`[` next/prev, selection synced tree ⇄ timeline ⇄ URL. The 681-LoC `trace-detail.tsx` is **decomposed** into a `trace-detail/` module dir + pure `src/lib/{trace-layout,otel-attributes}.ts` (every file ≤500 LoC; the old file is a D1 re-export shim so all prior importers resolve). Full dashboard suite green (1463 passed, 8 pre-existing skips); `tsc`/`eslint` clean; production `vite` build compiles the new deps. Live-verified through the real `/v1/dashboard/lens` BFF on a 6-span multi-subagent Claude Code trace (all 6 DoD, subagent-group collapse + PII fail-closed proven in the browser). Per-fix mapping in the spec docstring closes Red Team #40 #50 #51 #55 #56 #73 #74 #75 #76 #78 #82 #83 #96 #97 #98 #102 #121 #124 #128. **LIVE dev-public parity + DELETE of the 24 source `.sh` files stays halt-boundary per M12 Q2** (Class B sponsor JUNTOS per `cleanroom-up-live-test-mandate.md` INQUEBRÁVEL).
