## [0.22.1] - 2026-07-05

### Fixed
- **BFF: lens dashboard client timeout 10s→35s so the LLM-backed Ask (M33) + replay (M55) paths stop 502-ing (theo-lens live verification).** The `/v1/dashboard/lens` `DataClient` also proxies the two LLM-backed paths — `POST .../ask` and `POST .../replay` — whose downstream chat client caps at 30s (theo-lens `CHAT_TIMEOUT_MS`). The client's `http.Client{Timeout: 10s}` raced that call: a legitimate copilot ask over a real trace exceeded 10s and surfaced as `502 "context deadline exceeded (Client.Timeout exceeded while awaiting headers)"` — the failure caught during the M42/M55 live browser verification against a real provider (OpenRouter). Raised to 35s (30s downstream + margin), mirroring the themory LLM path (30s). Fast paths (traces list / cost / saved-views) are unaffected — the timeout is only a ceiling. The M53 alert-evaluator client (metrics-only, no LLM) stays at 10s. Live-proven end-to-end: Ask → grounded answer; replay → real output+tokens+latency (both `200`). `cmd/main.go`.

## [0.22.0] - 2026-07-05

### Added
- **M7 tenant blob storage (WIP — foundation layer):** `internal/storage/` vertical foundation: `s3x` fork-and-own S3 client + SigV4 signer (validated byte-identical to pocketbase reference vectors; zero aws-sdk), `caps.go` per-tier quotas (Starter 100MB/25MB · Pro 5GB/100MB · Team 25GB/500MB · Enterprise Unlimited/5GB, fail-safe to starter), `share/` HMAC capability tokens (constant-time verify, tamper/path-swap/rotation-tested), `service.go` ObjectStore DIP seam + `KeyForTenant` server-side tenant-namespacing (EC-1 cross-tenant byte isolation), config all-or-none validation. All `go test -race` GREEN. Blueprint ADRs D1/D2/D4. Plan `.claude/knowledge-base/plans/tenant-blob-storage-plan.md` (SHIPPABLE 95.6).
- **BFF: `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces` limit clamp raised 100→500 (theo-lens M56).** The M56 export pages at `limit=500` (theo-lens `LIST_TRACES_LIMIT_CAP`); the old `pageParams` clamp (`≤100`) silently dropped `limit=500` → theo-lens defaulted to the newest 50, so the export truncated to 50 rows and disclosed "complete" (a silent truncation the pre-merge review caught). The clamp is now the theo-lens cap (500); a value above it is dropped (theo-lens 400s on it), never silently reduced. `offset` is already forwarded. 2 handler tests (limit=500+offset=500 forwarded; over-cap 501 dropped).
- **Dashboard: Observability traces export — CSV + JSON (theo-lens M56).** The traces list gains an **Export CSV** / **Export JSON** action that downloads the **currently-filtered** set (honors the M49/M52 filters — WYSIWYG; omitted when the set is empty), **bounded by a documented cap** (`EXPORT_CAP=5000`, paged over the API's 500/request `limit`) with an **honest disclosure** ("Exported N traces" / "cap reached — narrow filters") — **no silent truncation** (a failure mode every SOTA peer has; the deep-research pass found none disclose). **No new dependency, no new backend** (Rule 9): the export reuses the existing `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces` filter contract via the same `buildTraceQuery`, and the shipped CSV/download machinery — the **CWE-1236 formula-injection guard** + RFC-4180 escaper (`neutralizeFormula`/`csvField`) and the Blob+anchor+deferred-revoke download are **extracted into shared `lib/csv.ts` + `lib/download.ts`** (one source, reused by the audit export + traces — the audit export is refactored onto them, behavior unchanged). CSV is **injection-safe** (a `=HYPERLINK(...)` in a user-authored span field is apostrophe-neutralized before RFC escaping — a guard **no peer ships**, verified against langfuse/opik/phoenix/laminar); JSON is the **raw machine-faithful records** (export == API). Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/data-export-read-api-blueprint.md` (ADRs D1/D2/D3). Pairs with theo-lens `docs/lens-read-api.md` (the documented `GET /v1/traces` read API a customer pulls with a `theo_live_*` key). Tests: `csv.ts` primitives (guard + RFC-4180 + `toCsv`) + `traces-export.ts` (`tracesToCsv` columns + injection-guard, `tracesToJson` raw shape, `fetchFilteredTraces` complete/capped/query-honoring) + traces-list component (Export CSV/JSON download honoring filters, cap disclosure, empty-omit, fetch-error) + the existing `audit-csv.test` stays green after the refactor. `tsc`/`eslint`/`vite build` + full dashboard suite (1363) green; the export markers verified in the real `dist/` bundle. `/deps-audit` no-op PASS. Live browser verification (the actual file download) deferred (Chrome debug-port block).
- **Dashboard: Observability prompt playground / span replay (theo-lens M55).** From an LLM span in the trace-detail view, a user opens a **playground**: the span's input (messages + model) is pre-filled and **editable**, and a **Run** re-executes it against the **real provider** server-side, showing the new **output + tokens + latency** next to the original — closing the prompt-iteration loop in-product. **No new dependency**: the provider call reuses theo-lens's sanctioned M33 copilot path via a new `POST /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}/replay` **BFF proxy** (copying the `Ask` per-tenant Model-B body-proxy — the browser never holds a provider key; a foreign/missing trace is refused upstream). The replay is **ephemeral** — it persists **nothing** to the immutable trace (the M44 read-only-body invariant); the UX labels it explicitly a **new run** ("the original trace is unchanged"). An opt-in **"Save as dataset item"** is the only persist path, reusing the M44 `AddDatasetItem`. Messages are edited via controlled inputs (role `Select` + content `Textarea` per message — **plain text, never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`**) and the model output is rendered as **plain text** (XSS-safe). Budget-honest: a provider outage / rate-limit / unconfigured provider surfaces a **typed error**, never a silent hang. Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/prompt-playground-span-replay-blueprint.md` (ADRs D1/D2/D3). Tests: BFF route-registration + fail-closed-401 + no-body-mutation route audit for the replay proxy; component (edit→run→output, tokens+latency+typed-error, no-mutation-of-original-panel, cross-tenant refusal, save-as-dataset). Pairs with the theo-lens `POST /v1/traces/:id/replay` endpoint (usage-capturing provider call + rate-limited handler). `go build/vet/test` + full dashboard suite + `tsc`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the playground markers verified in the real `dist/` bundle. `/deps-audit` no-op PASS. Live browser verification against the real provider is the one documented manual step (Chrome debug-port block).
- **Dashboard: Observability trace/run compare + diff (theo-lens M54).** A user picks **two traces** (multi-select in the traces list) and opens a **compare view** — **side-by-side timelines** (reusing the M50 waterfall helpers per lane) + a **metrics delta table** (duration / cost / tokens / error, per-trace and per-span where alignable) that highlights deltas **honestly** (faster/slower green, cheaper/costlier). Frontend-heavy, **no new dependency, no engine change**: the span tree (`GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}`) already carries timing/status/tokens, and theo-lens already serves per-trace **cost** at `GET /v1/traces/:id/cost` (M5) — so the only backend surface is a **thin BFF proxy** `GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}/cost` copying the sibling `GetTrace` handler (same per-tenant Model-B path + 404 isolation — no client-side cost math, no fabrication; ADR D2). The **honesty keystone** is span alignment: a pure `alignSpanTrees(a,b)` matches spans by **name + kind + sibling-ordinal path**; when structures diverge, unmatched spans render as **"only in A / only in B"** with the delta **suppressed** and the header marked "structurally different" — never a fabricated 1:1 diff (the deep-research pass confirmed **no peer** ships a two-trace span-tree diff; the alignment is genuinely novel — ADR D1). Delta math is a pure `numericDelta(current, baseline)` (absolute + direction + percent, **null on equal or missing side** — Langfuse `calculateNumericDiff` idiom), rendered via `@usetheo/ui` `Badge` with a `lowerIsBetter` variant. Entry is URL-driven (`/observability/compare?ids=A,B`, first id = baseline — Phoenix/Opik precedent), shareable; each lane is an **isolated `useQuery`** (tree + cost) so a missing/foreign trace shows a per-lane error without sinking the page. Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-run-compare-blueprint.md` (ADRs D1/D2/D3). Tests: BFF route-registration + fail-closed-401 for the cost proxy; pure `numericDelta` (equal/greater/less/missing) + `alignSpanTrees` (identical/one-extra/renamed/reordered) + `traceMetrics`; component (two-lane render, one-missing-trace error, cross-tenant refusal, delta highlighting faster/cheaper, divergent-structure honesty, cost-degraded) + traces-list two-select → Compare entry. `go build/vet/test` + full dashboard suite + `tsc`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the compare markers verified in the real `dist/` bundle. `/deps-audit` no-op PASS. Live browser verification deferred (Chrome debug-port block).
- **Observability threshold alerting (theo-lens M53).** A user defines a **threshold rule** — metric ∈ {error_rate, cost, p95_latency}, comparator ∈ {gt, gte, lt, lte}, threshold, window, target webhook — and a **background evaluator** checks it per-tenant against the M51 observability series; on a breach it **dispatches a notification through the existing J-16 SSRF-guarded webhook delivery worker** (reuse, not reinvent — Rule 9). Spans three layers, no new dependency: **(1) engine migration `000054_lens_alert_rules`** — a per-tenant rule table (`metric`/`comparator`/`threshold`/`window_seconds`/`webhook_id`→`workspace_webhooks` + anti-storm state columns `status`/`last_evaluated_at`/`last_triggered_at`/`renotify_interval_seconds`), CHECK-constrained enums, `(tenant_id, subject)` + partial-enabled indexes; **(2) theo-cloud** — a `LensAlertRulesHandler` CRUD (`POST`/`GET`/`DELETE`/`PATCH` under `/v1/dashboard/lens/alert-rules`, `subject`+`tenant_id` scoped from the Principal — never the body — enum-validated, `lar-{16hex}` ids, the target `webhook_id` **ownership-validated** — it must be the caller tenant's own active webhook, so a rule can never POST this tenant's alerts to another tenant's endpoint — **cross-tenant delete → 404-not-403**) + a **`lens_alert_evaluator`** worker (ticks over enabled rules, mints a per-tenant **`observability:read`** key via the lens Broker — Model B, least-privilege: the platform-admin key stays control-plane — fetches the series, and on a fire atomically wins the transition (`UPDATE ... WHERE status='OK'`) + INSERTs one `workspace_webhook_deliveries` row for the running J-16 worker to POST — the flip + INSERT run in ONE transaction so a failed enqueue rolls back the flip and the next tick retries, never a silently-lost alert); **(3) dashboard** — a fixed alert-rule form (metric/comparator/threshold/window/webhook `Select`s) + a rules list with a live **status badge** (OK / TRIGGERED) + last-triggered + enable-toggle + delete. The **anti-storm keystone** is a **fire-once-until-cleared** state machine (langfuse `applyStateMachine` — a metric breached for an hour fires once, not once per tick; an opt-in renotify interval re-fires; the conditional `UPDATE` is the multi-replica single-source-of-truth guard, no Redis — ADR D1). A **closed-enum rule referencing a registered webhook** (the reused worker JOINs `workspace_webhooks`), not a generic builder or a channel abstraction (ADR D2/YAGNI). The evaluator's per-tenant fetch is the sanctioned Model-B broker path, not a standing all-tenant key (ADR D3). Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-threshold-alerting-blueprint.md`. Tests: pure `evaluateBreach`/`metricFromSeries`/`nextAlertState` table tests (all comparator/metric/state cases + **N-breached-ticks-fire-once**) + evaluator sqlmock (breach → atomic UPDATE + one delivery INSERT; still-breached → no re-INSERT; lost transition → no INSERT; fetch-error → skip tenant) + 7 CRUD sqlmock (create `lar-`, list scoped, **cross-tenant delete → 404**, enum-400, malformed-id-400, toggle) + 7 dashboard component (list + status badge, create POST, toggle PATCH, delete, webhook-gate disclosure, create-disabled-until-complete). `go build/vet/test` + full dashboard suite (1315) + `tsc -b`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the alert-rules markers in the real `dist/` bundle; the migration DDL validated against real Postgres (`54/u` applied). `/deps-audit` no-op PASS. **Prerequisites:** the engine migration `000054` runs before the evaluator/handler work (cross-repo ordering — J-16 precedent), and a customer must have a registered webhook (the delivery target — the DoD's chosen channel); the form discloses this when none exist. Live browser verification deferred (Chrome debug-port block).
- **Dashboard: Observability saved views + advanced trace filters (theo-lens M52).** The traces list gains **advanced filters** — cost range, token range, and session — and **saved views** (name + save the current filter combination, re-apply it, delete it; per-tenant, shareable via URL). Spans four layers, no new dependency: **(1) theo-lens core** — `listTracesOptionsSchema` + `listTraces` SQL gain `minCostUsd`/`maxCostUsd`/`minTokens`/`maxTokens`/`sessionId` as trace-level column predicates (`traces.cost_usd`/`total_tokens`/`session_id` — `gte`/`lte`/`eq`, `!= null` so a `0` bound is honored, `min > max` fails soft to empty), boundary-validated (`z.coerce` NaN → 400); **(2) engine migration `000053_lens_saved_views`** — a per-tenant table (`id`/`subject`/`tenant_id`/`name`/`query_string`/`created_at` + `(tenant_id, subject)` index) mirroring the J-16 `workspace_webhooks` tenancy (hard delete — a saved view is disposable preference); **(3) theo-cloud BFF** — the M49 `traceFilter` whitelist extends to forward the 5 new params (an arbitrary param stays structurally unforwardable), plus a new `LensSavedViewsHandler` CRUD (`POST`/`GET`/`DELETE` under `/v1/dashboard/lens/saved-views`, `subject`+`tenant_id` scoped from the Principal — never the body — with a cross-tenant delete collapsing to **404-not-403**, the existence-leak keystone; raw `database/sql`; `lsv-{16hex}` ids); **(4) dashboard** — a fixed advanced-filter row (cost/token min-max number inputs + session text, nuqs-backed + URL-persisted) + a saved-views bar (apply pushes the stored query string onto the URL → nuqs re-hydrates; a name form to save the current filter URL; per-view delete via `ConfirmDialog`). Saved views store the **nuqs query string verbatim** — theo-lens's filters already live in the URL (M49), so the query string IS the view (opik's model), applied by writing the URL, shared as the URL itself — no structured-config or permalink-minting machinery (blueprint ADR D1). A fixed filter row, **not a generic filter-builder** (YAGNI for a fixed column set — ADR D2). **user/actor filter is out of scope** (the trace data model has no user column — session is the actor grouping; an attribute scan would be a fragile workaround — ADR D3). Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-saved-views-advanced-filters-blueprint.md`. Tests: theo-lens 2 schema-unit + 4 real-Postgres integration (each filter narrows; inverted range → empty) + 1 API-boundary (400 + forwards); theo-cloud 1 advanced-filter-forward + 7 saved-views sqlmock (create `lsv-`, list scoped to `(subject, tenant_id)`, **cross-tenant delete → 404**, 401, malformed-id 400, empty-name 400, 204 on owned delete); dashboard 2 `buildTraceQuery` units + 6 component (cost/session filter narrows, saved-views list/apply/save/delete round-trip, save-disabled-until-named). theo-lens `pnpm test`(contract) + integration (real PG) + theo-cloud `go build/vet/test` + full dashboard suite (1308) + `tsc -b`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the saved-views + advanced-filter markers verified in the real `dist/` bundle; the migration DDL validated against real Postgres (pg-cloud, rolled-back). `/deps-audit` no-op PASS. **Prerequisite:** the engine migration `000053` must run before the saved-views handler works (cross-repo ordering — J-16 precedent). Live browser verification deferred (Chrome debug-port block).
- **Dashboard: Observability time-series analytics (theo-lens M51).** The Observability Overview gains **trends over time** — Throughput, Error-rate (%), Latency (p50/p95/p99 overlaid), and **Cost by model** — over a selectable, URL-persisted range (1h/24h/7d/30d), alongside the KPI tiles (kept). The data-layer audit was decisive: theo-lens `GET /v1/metrics/observability?range=` **already returns fully bucketed series server-side** (SQL `date_trunc`; `packages/core/src/domain/observability.ts` — `traffic[]`/`latency[]`/`tokensByModel[]`), and `lens.tsx` already fetched them (it only plotted KPI tiles). So M51 is (1) a tiny **BFF range-forward** slice (`Observability(ctx, range)` + a validated `range` **whitelist** — the M49 pattern; a bogus/arbitrary range is structurally unforwardable, theo-lens applies its 24h default) and (2) a frontend that plots the series. **No charting dependency added** despite all four SOTA peers using Recharts: they use it to power *configurable chart/widget builders*, a generality theo-lens's fixed metric set stays below (blueprint ADR D1) — instead a small **pure-SVG `MetricTrendChart`** (the `@usetheo/ui` `TokenUsageChart` idiom) with pure, unit-tested scale/path helpers (`linScale`/`niceMax`/`seriesPath`), a color legend, and a visually-hidden a11y `<table>` (screen-reader parity). The by-model chart pivots `tokensByModel[]` to one line per model with an **"Others" collapse** for high cardinality (opik convergence); an empty window renders an explicit **empty-state**, never a fabricated zero line (phoenix convergence); all bucketing stays **server-side** (the range re-fetches, never client re-buckets). Render is SVG geometry + text only — never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`. Blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-timeseries-analytics-blueprint.md` (ADRs D1/D2). 3 BFF Go tests (range forwarded / omitted / handler-whitelists-and-drops-`evil`) + 8 `metric-trend-chart` cases (helpers + path-per-series + single-bucket dot + empty-state + a11y table) + 6 `lens-series` pivot cases (throughput/error-%/latency/by-model + Others collapse + zero-fill) + 6 `lens.test.tsx` cases (four charts render, one-line-per-model, range re-fetches with `?range=`, empty-state, KPI no-regression). `go build/vet/test` + full dashboard suite + `tsc -b`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the `trend-line`/`trend-chart` markers verified present in the real `dist/` bundle. `/deps-audit` no-op PASS (no dependency delta). Live browser verification deferred (Chrome debug-port block; lens host service not running this session) — the series are fed by the `GET /v1/metrics/observability` endpoint proven live in M40/M42.
- **Dashboard: Observability trace waterfall / timeline span view (theo-lens M50).** The trace-detail view gains the **signature observability UX** it still lacked: each visible span now renders a **timeline bar** positioned by its start-offset (relative to the trace) and sized by its duration — so a user *sees* sequence, overlap (parallel LLM calls), and where wall-clock time went, not only an indented tree. Frontend-only and **zero new dependency, zero theo-lens/BFF change**: the data (`startTimeUnixNano`/`endTimeUnixNano` per span) was already fetched + flattened (M47/M48). The bar layout is a **pure, unit-tested helper** `computeBarLayout(spanStart, spanEnd, traceStart, traceEnd) → {leftPct, widthPct, unbounded}` — BigInt-ns arithmetic, div-by-zero guard, clamp `[0,100]` with `left+width ≤ 100`, missing-end → zero-width `unbounded` dashed bar; trace bounds are `min(start)`/`max(end)` over ALL spans (`computeTraceBounds`, memoized once). The lane lives **inside the shared `SpanRow`**, so both the recursive and the M48-virtualized paths get the waterfall for free and clicking a bar selects the same span the tree row would (row-level selection — no second interactive element to collide with the tree's a11y name). Bars are colored with an **error override** (`statusCode !== '0'` → destructive) and floored to a 2px min-width so a sub-ms span stays visible; render is styles+text only (never `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` — the XSS guard holds). Copies the pattern all four SOTA peers converge on — hand-rolled divs+`%`, **no charting library** (langfuse/phoenix/opik/laminar all hand-roll; blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-timeline-waterfall-blueprint.md`, ADRs D1/D2). 13 new `trace-detail.test.tsx` cases (6 `computeBarLayout` units incl. clamp/zero-negative-duration/missing-end-unbounded/zero-trace-window/parallel-overlap; 2 `computeTraceBounds`; 5 component: bar-per-visible-span, bar-style-reflects-layout, bar-click-selects, error-marked, unbounded-marked) + the M47/M48/M49 suites stay green; full dashboard suite (1281) + `tsc -b`/`eslint`/`vite build` green; the `span-bar` marker is verified present in the real `dist/` bundle. `/deps-audit` is a no-op PASS (no dependency delta). Live browser verification deferred (known Chrome debug-port block; lens host service not running this session) — the timeline is fed by the exact `GET /v1/traces/:id` data proven live in M40/M42.
- **Dashboard: Observability trace search & filters (theo-lens M49).** The `/observability/traces` list gains a **search box** (trace name + full-text span content) and a **filter row** — status (`Errors only`), model, and time range (`1h`/`24h`/`7d`) — that narrow the list **server-side**. Zero new theo-lens work and zero new BFF query logic reinvented: theo-lens's `/v1/traces` already implements the filter SQL (`status`/`model`/`from`/`q` full-text via `websearch_to_tsquery`), so the slice is (1) a small **param-forwarding slice** in the BFF (`internal/thelens/data_client.go` `ListTraces(TraceListFilter)` + `internal/routes/lens_dashboard.go` `traceFilter` **whitelist** — only `status`/`model`/`from`/`to`/`q`/`limit`/`offset` are forwarded; an arbitrary upstream param like `evil=1` is structurally unforwardable, not merely stripped) and (2) a frontend UI on the **already-installed `nuqs`** — a redundant dep is not added (parsimony rung-4). Filters are **URL-persisted** (`?q=&status=&model=&range=`) so a filtered view is shareable/bookmarkable, and a filter-specific empty state ("No traces match these filters") is distinct from the cold "No traces yet". The search box **debounces at 300 ms** (a full-text search fires per pause, not per keystroke). The query key is the filter **values** (not the derived `from` timestamp) so the cache is stable across renders and `buildTraceQuery` computes `from` from the clock only at fetch time (no refetch loop; the helper takes an injected clock and is unit-tested deterministically). Borrows the server-side-filter + URL-state architecture the four SOTA peers converge on (langfuse/phoenix/opik/laminar), per the blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-search-filters-blueprint.md`. 3 BFF Go tests (data-client forwards the whitelist / omits empties; handler whitelists params + drops `evil`) + 14 `traces.test.tsx` cases (6 `buildTraceQuery` unit cases; search→`q=`, each filter→its param, `?q=` URL round-trip, filter-empty-state, and a negative "no filter params on the cold load"); the full dashboard suite (1231) stays green, `tsc -b`/`vite build` green, and the M49 search/filter strings are verified present in the real `dist/` bundle. URL-round-trip tests use nuqs's official testing adapter to isolate searchParams (the router adapter shares a throttle queue across tests). No new dependency; no theo-lens change; no mutating verb added.
- **Activate the `rag:*` customer key scope.** The RAG vertical (theo-rag, M12-M20) is now ISSUABLE by customers: `internal/routes/api_keys.go` `validScopes` activates `rag:read`/`rag:write`/`rag:delete` (graduated from the reserved `rag:*`), and the API Tokens dashboard (`dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.tsx`) offers them as ACTIVE scopes. `rag:admin` is intentionally NOT issuable (broker-held key-lifecycle, least privilege). A customer can now create a `theo_live_*` key with `rag:*` in the dashboard and use it against the unified `/v1/rag` edge + `/mcp/rag` — the `forward-auth-rag` broker already intersects consumer scopes least-privilege, so no broker change. Closes #168. (#168)
- **M1 J-06 — Overview KPI strip + the `plan_price` billing observation (backend + frontend).** The authenticated home now renders a four-KPI strip (Projects / Deploys 7d / Active envs / Plan) at a glance. Per MEET-2026-07-03-003 D1 (theo ADR `2026-07-03-j06-overview-backend-read-model-boundaries`): the Stripe webhook (`internal/billing/webhook.go`) now parses `price.unit_amount`+`currency` and persists them onto the subscription row, and `GET /v1/billing` emits an additive nested `plan_price` object observed from that row — no hardcoded Go price constant (kills the #157 drift class), no live Stripe call on the hot read path. The KPI is honestly labeled **"Plan"** (the recurring list price), NOT "Spend MTD"; `plan_price` is omitted for enterprise / not-yet-persisted plans and the strip renders "—" (never a fabricated $0). New `useOverviewKPIs` aggregator (composes projects + `/v1/billing` + the engine `/api/v1/builds/stats` deploys aggregate), `useBuildStats`, and `OverviewKPIStrip` component. 20 tests: webhook persist (2) + `/v1/billing` `plan_price` (5) + aggregator (6) + strip (9, incl. `formatPlanPrice`); tsc + eslint green; overview page test 13/13. Requires the theo engine migration `000049` to run first.
- **Billing: invoice history on the dashboard (J-13).** The Billing page gains an owner-only "Invoices" card backed by a new BFF endpoint `GET /v1/billing/invoices` (theo-cloud). The endpoint resolves the Stripe customer SERVER-SIDE from the caller's own tenant (never a client param), lists the 100 most-recent invoices, and re-shapes each into Theo conventions: `amount_minor` (integer minor units from the invoice total, never a float) + `currency`, ISO-8601 UTC timestamps, `status`, and `hosted_invoice_url` — the Stripe-hosted invoice page (the raw `invoice_pdf` bearer link is deliberately NOT exposed, a capability-URL PII decision). Owner-gated (`RequireRole "owner"`, a member gets 403 and the card is hidden); a Stripe outage returns a typed 502 (never a silent empty list) and degrades ONLY the invoice card (failure isolation — plan/usage keep rendering); an unsubscribed tenant / absent customer fails closed to an empty list. 5s fail-fast timeout, `no-store`, and a log-scrub denylist (customer id / URLs never logged). Design ratified in a T2 review (`docs/meetings/records/MEET-2026-07-03-004.md`). Tests: Go adapter (httptest raw-HTTP parse) + 6 handler cases (owner/empty/IDOR-fail-closed/zero/502-scrubbed/401) + frontend money-format (`2900 → $29.00`) + invoice-card states (rows / empty / isolated-error / 403-hidden); `go build`/`vet`/`test` + `vitest`/`tsc`/`eslint` green.
- **Dashboard: Status KPI strip wired to live metrics (J-18 frontend).** The Status page's KPI strip previously hard-coded `null` for the 4 Prometheus-backed KPIs (uptime 24h, p95 latency, error rate, RPS) — rendering "—". They now derive their current value from the `metrics_proxy` endpoint via a new `useKpiMetric(name, env, signal, multiplier)` hook (fetches a 1h range, takes the last datapoint × the unit multiplier: uptime `avg_over_time(up)` 0..1 → % ×100; p95 seconds → ms ×1000; error_rate already a % (theo#170 fix) ×1; rps ×1). The 4 hooks are called at the top of the component (rules-of-hooks refactor — they were previously derived after the early returns). They keep rendering "—" honestly until a Prometheus backend is reachable (`THEO_PROMETHEUS_ADDRESS`). New `use-kpi-metric.ts` (5 derivation tests: latest-point × multiplier, unit conversions, null-on-empty/NaN) + a project-status wiring test (metric 99.5 → "99.50%"); `vitest` + `tsc` + `eslint` green. Pairs with the engine PRs theo#169 (concrete Prometheus client) + theo#171 (error_rate unit fix).
- **Dashboard: "Deploy" primary CTA on the project Overview (J-08).** The project's center page (`/projects/:name`) previously had no way to trigger a deploy from the UI — the operator had to drill into the Deployments sub-journey. It now surfaces a `PageShell.primaryAction` "Deploy" button (top-right, Vercel-canonical) that reuses the existing `RedeployDialog` with the current active-release build as source (clone last-good `commit_sha` + environment + apps_spec, submit a fresh build). The source build is `status.active_release.build_id` with a fallback to the latest build; on a cold project (no builds) the CTA is omitted, matching the Deployments page where the button is disabled with nothing to clone. Contained in `pages/project-detail.tsx` — no new route, no backend, no contract change. 2 new tests (renders + opens dialog with active build; absent on cold project); `vitest` + `tsc` + `eslint` green.
- **Dashboard: "Export CSV" on the Audit log (J-14).** The audit log had no way to export — closing the DoD's "export CSV → arquivo válido" gap. A `PageShell.primaryAction` "Export CSV" button downloads the currently-visible rows (WYSIWYG — honors the active action/actor/source filters; omitted when the view is empty) as an RFC 4180 CSV via the canonical Blob + anchor pattern (mirrors `lib/log-download.ts`, incl. the deferred `revokeObjectURL` so Safari/Firefox finish streaming). New `lib/audit-csv.ts` — `auditEntriesToCsv` emits machine-faithful RAW columns (`Timestamp,Actor,Action,Resource Type,Resource ID,Severity,Correlation ID`) with proper quote/comma/newline escaping, so the file pivots cleanly in a spreadsheet rather than carrying the humanized table strings. **Security hardening (pre-merge adversarial review — theo-cloud#157):** a CSV formula-injection guard (CWE-1236 / OWASP "CSV Injection") neutralizes any cell whose first char is `=`/`+`/`-`/`@`/tab/CR with a leading apostrophe before RFC escaping — audit fields are user-authored (project names, emails, resource ids) and the export is opened by a privileged owner, so `=HYPERLINK(...)`/`@…`/DDE payloads would otherwise execute as live formulas in Excel/Sheets. 9 tests (the original 6 + 3 injection cases: leading-formula-char neutralization, a realistic HYPERLINK exfil payload, benign-value-unchanged regression); `vitest` + `tsc` + `eslint` green. No backend, no route, no contract change.
- **Dashboard + BFF: workspace webhook delivery history (J-16 slice 3, customer visibility).** New owner-gated `GET /v1/settings/webhooks/{id}/deliveries` (`WorkspaceWebhooksHandler.Deliveries`) returns the most-recent 10 deliveries for one webhook — id, event type, status, attempt count, last HTTP status, last error, timestamps — **scoped to the authenticated owner** (`subject` + `tenant_id` join on the parent webhook, so a caller can't read another tenant's history), error messages truncated + control-chars stripped. Reuses the shared `DeliveryView` shape. The `/settings/webhooks` page gains a per-row **View deliveries** button that opens a Sheet listing the delivery history with a status badge (delivered/pending/delivering=warning, dead_letter=destructive), attempt count, and any error — populates once the delivery pipeline is live, empty-states until then. 3 BFF tests (401 unauth · malformed-id 400 · list scoped-to-owner via sqlmock) + 1 dashboard test (View-deliveries opens the sheet + fetches the scoped endpoint); `go build`/routes tests + `tsc`/`eslint`/`vitest` (6/6) + production build green.
- **Workspace webhook delivery worker: dispatch deploy events to customer URLs (J-16 slice 3, delivery — the final stage).** New `internal/workspace_webhook_worker` background dispatcher (started in `cmd/main.go`) drains `workspace_webhook_deliveries` (engine migration 000052) and POSTs each to the customer-registered URL through the **SSRF-guarded `safehttp` client** (rejects the resolved destination IP if internal, no redirects — #149). REUSES the memory worker's exported, tested primitives (`Dispatch`/`NextBackoff`/`IsTerminalAttempt`/HMAC signing) so signing + retry + the single HTTP attempt are not duplicated (DRY), and ADDS the two things the memory worker lacks (per the slice-3 distributed-systems review): a per-attempt **lease** (`claimed_at`/`claimed_by`) and a **visibility-timeout reaper** that resets rows stuck in `delivering` past the lease — closing the permanent `delivering`-orphan class a crashed worker would otherwise leave. Claim is multi-replica-safe (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`); retry is exponential-backoff → `dead_letter` after 9 attempts; the lease is cleared on every terminal/retry transition. A classified SSRF-rejection observer feeds `theo_workspace_webhook_ssrf_rejected_total{reason}` so any internal-target dial attempt surfaces in metrics (MEET-2026-07-03-005 wiring triad). **Pre-merge hardening (adversarial review — theo-cloud#155/#156):** (a) the claim batch is capped at the in-flight concurrency so a batch-tail row can never expire its lease while queued (which would let the reaper reclaim + re-dispatch a row this worker still holds → concurrent double-delivery); (b) `recordResult` is guarded on `(claimed_by, status='delivering')` — a stale worker whose lease was reaped mid-dispatch has its write dropped, never clobbering the new owner (no lost update / resurrection); (c) the reaper now counts the orphaned attempt + backs off + dead-letters at the cap (previously it reset `next_attempt_at=NOW()` uncounted → poison rows tight-looped and never dead-lettered); (d) the SSRF guard additionally blocks CGNAT `100.64.0.0/10` (AWS EKS pod CIDR), NAT64 `64:ff9b::/96`, and RFC2544/protocol/Class-E ranges that Go's `IsPrivate()` omits. Tests: 9 real-Postgres worker tests (the original 6 + lease-lost-drops-stale-write + reaper-counts-attempt-and-backs-off + reaper-dead-letters-at-cap) + the safehttp CGNAT/NAT64 cases, all GREEN against an ephemeral PG; whole-module `go build ./...` + `go vet` green. Completes the emit→queue→fan-out→dispatch pipeline.
- **Deploy-event enqueuer: fan out the `deploy_events` outbox to webhook deliveries (J-16 slice 3, consumer).** New `internal/deploy_event_enqueuer` background loop (started in `cmd/main.go` alongside the memory-webhook dispatcher) drains the engine's `deploy_events` outbox (migration 000051) and, for each event, inserts one `workspace_webhook_deliveries` (000052) row per active `workspace_webhooks` (000050) subscribed to that event type for the event's tenant — then marks the outbox row drained. Multi-replica-safe (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` on the outbox), idempotent (`ON CONFLICT (webhook_id, transition_id) DO NOTHING` — re-draining never double-enqueues), and tenant-isolated (a tenant's event only reaches that tenant's webhooks). Pure DB fan-out — **no outbound HTTP**, so no SSRF surface (that is the delivery worker's concern). Per the ratified design (MEET-2026-07-03-005, Option C) the fan-out lives in theo-cloud, not the engine (decoupling invariant). Tests: 4 real-Postgres tests (fan-out creates a `pending` delivery + marks drained + idempotent re-tick; unsubscribed event → 0; revoked webhook → 0; cross-tenant event → 0 — the #106/#111 isolation class) against an ephemeral PG (4/4 GREEN); `go build ./...` + `go vet` green.
- **Dashboard: Observability trace viewer at scale — virtualization + rich rendering (theo-lens M48).** Scales the M47 trace-detail view to large traces and rich payloads: (1) the span tree **virtualizes above a threshold** (≈200 rows) using the dashboard's ALREADY-installed `@tanstack/react-virtual` — **zero new dep for virtualization** (parsimony rung-4 reuse; small traces keep M47's proven recursive render, so the common path is unregressed); (2) JSON/text span values render in a **read-only, syntax-highlighted, foldable CodeMirror** (`@uiw/react-codemirror` + `@codemirror/lang-json`) — the M47 guards are preserved (chat-message arrays still role-render, `"<REDACTED>"` is still labelled, oversized values still truncate with show-more, and it **still never uses `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`** — CodeMirror renders the value as document text, not markup); (3) the tree/detail split is **resizable** (`react-resizable-panels`, `autoSaveId` persistence). Folds in the two residual M47 LOW findings (stable keys under a duplicate `spanId`; an error badge for a non-zero `statusCode`). Only **two new deps** (CodeMirror + resizable-panels; virtualization reused) — `/deps-audit` PASS (OSV: 0 CVEs on all three libs). Borrows the exact configs the four SOTA peers converge on (langfuse `VirtualizedTree`/`tree-flattening`, phoenix/opik `@uiw/react-codemirror`, opik `ResizablePanelGroup`), per the blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-viewer-scale-rich-blueprint.md`. 21 `trace-detail.test.tsx` cases (adds: below/above virtualization threshold, resizable-split handle, error badge, duplicate-spanId key stability, `flattenVisible` collapse/depth, JSON-in-read-only-CodeMirror) + the M47 suite stays green; `tsc`/`eslint`/`vitest` (full dashboard suite) green. react-resizable-panels is mocked at the test boundary (jsdom cannot lay out a layout lib); the drag behaviour is verified live.
- **Dashboard: Observability trace-detail view — SOTA span explorer (theo-lens M47).** Clicking a trace in `/observability/traces` now opens a real **trace-detail view** instead of only a tags/annotations panel: a new `dashboard/src/pages/lens/trace-detail.tsx` (`LensTraceDetail`) fetches the reconstructed span tree from the read BFF (`GET /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{id}` → theo-lens `GET /v1/traces/:id`) and renders a two-pane — left = the recursive **span tree** (name, duration, model badge, in→out token counts, error status, click-to-select); right = the **selected span's detail** (input/output, attributes, events, and model/provider/tokens/ttft/latency). Input/output are **escaped-rendered** — chat-JSON is pretty-printed via `JSON.stringify`, `"<REDACTED>"` prompts are labelled as client-redacted (not shown as markup), and oversized values truncate with a show-more toggle; the view **never uses `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`** (a test asserts this — the XSS guard). Borrows the architecture the four SOTA peers converge on (langfuse/phoenix/laminar/opik: split-pane tree+detail, format-dispatch render) but adds **zero runtime deps** — recursive render (our agent traces are far below langfuse's 350-span virtualization threshold) with `@usetheo/ui` + `@tanstack/react-query` (blueprint `.claude/knowledge-base/discoveries/blueprints/trace-detail-view-blueprint.md`, plan D1). The M44 tags + annotations are preserved (additive). 15 `trace-detail.test.tsx` cases (tree render, span-select, expand/collapse, scroll-to-selected, redacted input, pretty-JSON output, chat-role render, attributes/events/metadata, oversized truncation + show-more reveal, no-`dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, BFF-error, empty trace, + 3 pure-helper unit tests) + a `traces.test.tsx` no-regression case (asserts the tree fetch is GET-only); `tsc`/`eslint`/`vitest` green.
- **Dashboard: Settings › Webhooks registration page (J-16 slice 4).** The `/settings/webhooks` route stops being a placeholder — new `dashboard/src/pages/settings/webhooks.tsx` (`WorkspaceWebhooksPage`) mirrors the memory-webhooks page for the deploy-event resource: an **Add webhook** dialog (URL + label + deploy/build event checkboxes — `deploy.succeeded`/`deploy.failed`/`build.succeeded`/`build.failed`), a `DataTable` list, and a type-to-confirm destructive delete, all against the `/v1/settings/webhooks` BFF endpoints. On create, the per-webhook HMAC signing secret is surfaced **exactly once** in a show-once dialog (Stripe pattern) so the customer can store it before delivery goes live. Because the delivery worker is a later T2-gated slice, a persistent **"Delivery coming soon"** `Alert` discloses honestly (Rule 3) that no events are dispatched yet — a customer never registers a hook expecting silent events that never fire. 5 page tests (banner disclosure · empty state · list from `/v1/settings/webhooks/list` · create shows secret once · delete confirm) + app route repoint (7 route tests still green); `tsc`/`eslint`/`vitest` green.
- **BFF: workspace/deploy webhook registration CRUD (J-16 slice 2).** New `WorkspaceWebhooksHandler` (`internal/routes/workspace_webhooks.go`) — a mirror of the approved `MemoryWebhooksHandler` for a new resource (webhooks that fire on **deploy events** rather than memory events). Owner-only routes under `/v1/settings/webhooks`: `POST /` (create — validates `http(s)://` URL + 1–64-char label + non-empty events; mints a per-webhook `whsec_` HMAC-SHA256 secret returned **once**; `wwh-{16hex}` id), `GET /list` (active webhooks scoped to `(subject, tenant_id)`, cap 100), `DELETE /{id}` (soft-delete via `revoked_at`; cross-tenant / non-existent / already-revoked all collapse to **404**, never 403 — existence-leak prevention). Reuses the shared package helpers (`randomHex16`, `webhookURLPattern`, `webhookLabelMax`) to avoid duplicating the memory-webhooks logic (DRY). Registration-only v0.1 per the same ADR D2 precedent memory_webhooks shipped at — the **delivery worker + deploy-event trigger is a separate T2-gated slice**. Persists to the `workspace_webhooks` table (engine migration 000050, theo#173). 7 handler tests (401 unauth · non-http URL 400 · empty events 400 · create returns wwh-+whsec- · list scoped-to-tenant · delete 404-on-zero-rows · malformed-id 400) via sqlmock; `go build`/`vet`/`test` green.
- **Dashboard: multi-select bulk delete on the Projects list (J-07 D4).** The Projects table gains a leading select-all header checkbox + per-row checkboxes (row-click still navigates; the checkbox `stopPropagation`s), a bulk action bar that appears once ≥1 project is selected, and a type-to-confirm `BulkDeleteProjectsDialog` (the confirm phrase is `delete N project(s)`; the destructive button stays disabled until it matches exactly). Deletion is **best-effort**: `useBulkDeleteProjects` loops `engineFetch(DELETE /api/v1/projects/{name}, { throwOnError: true })` per project and returns `{ succeeded, failed:[{name,error}] }` — a project that can't be deleted (e.g. an active deploy) is surfaced honestly (`Couldn't delete: <name>`) and the dialog stays open (success is never claimed on partial failure). New `use-bulk-delete-projects.ts` (6 tests) + `bulk-delete-projects-dialog.tsx` (6 tests, `<Alert intent="destructive" description={error} />`) + `projects.tsx` wiring (1 integration test); `vitest` + `tsc` green.
- **Activate the `observability:*` customer key scope + rename the edge/broker scope `lens:* → observability:*`.** The Observability vertical (theo-lens, M37-M46) is now ISSUABLE by customers: `internal/routes/api_keys.go` `validScopes` activates `observability:read/write/delete/admin` (graduated from the reserved `traces:*`), and the API Tokens dashboard (`dashboard/src/pages/api-keys.tsx`) offers them as ACTIVE scopes. The edge + broker + BFF scope strings renamed `lens:* → observability:*` (`internal/thelens/*`, `internal/routes/{forward_auth,lens_dashboard}.go`, `cmd/main.go`) so the forward-auth-lens gate requires `observability:read` and the dashboard resolver mints `observability:read+write+delete`. A customer can now create a `theo_live_*` key with `observability:*` in the dashboard and use it against the unified `/v1/lens` edge — validated E2E (dashboard-issued key → Claude-Code OTLP through the real Traefik edge → per-tenant span). Paths (`/v1/lens/*`, `/v1/dashboard/lens/*`) unchanged; only scope strings. `go build`/`go test ./internal/routes ./internal/thelens ./cmd` + dashboard `vitest`/`tsc` green; `make lens-up` re-mints `observability:platform-admin` green.
- **Dashboard: group Memory / RAG / Observability under one "Capabilities" section.** The main-nav previously wrapped each capability in its own single-item group whose header repeated the item label (a redundant "Memory / Memory", "RAG / RAG", "Observability / Observability" nesting). Merged the three into a single semantic `Capabilities` group (the Theo data-plane capabilities); each item still drills into its own submenu. Sidebar + smoke tests green; live re-verified in real Chrome.
- **Dashboard: the lens section is renamed to "Observability" (label + routes).** The user-facing capability name in the dashboard changes from **Lens** to **Observability**: the sidebar section header + drill-down item, every page title (`Lens · X` → `Observability · X`, Overview `Lens` → `Observability`), the Overview `PageShell` title + its load-error copy. The frontend routes move `/lens/*` → `/observability/*` (router + sidebar paths + `resolveActiveMenu` + the Overview's Explore-card / "Browse traces" hrefs; the route smoke test follows). Out of scope by design (unchanged): the BFF paths (`/v1/dashboard/lens/*`), the internal menu id / component names (`LensOverviewPage`, …) / file paths, and the `theo-lens` module + edge path (`/v1/lens`). Behavior unchanged; 43 lens+overview+smoke tests + `tsc` + `eslint` green; live re-verified in real Chrome (`/observability` renders the KPI strip; `/observability/analytics` renders the 4 MetricCards).
- **theo-lens dashboard design standardization (M46).** Standardized the M44 lens section to the theo-memory / theo-rag design pattern (Unbreakable Rule 9) — a design-critic pass scored the baseline at 74/100; all 5 findings fixed (none deferred). New **`dashboard/src/pages/lens.tsx`** Overview landing at `/lens` (mirroring `pages/rag.tsx`: a `StatTile` KPI strip aggregated from the lens observability + cost buckets — Traces / Error-rate % / Latency p95 / Cost — a Getting-started card, and an Explore grid of the 8 sub-pages); `/lens` now opens on the Overview (Traces moved to `/lens/traces` only) with a matching sidebar Overview item + updated route smoke test. Swapped hand-rolled UI for `@usetheo/ui` primitives (Rule 9 / DRY): `lens/analytics.tsx` uses `MetricCard` (and now correctly **aggregates** theo-lens's bucketed observability/cost shape instead of reading non-existent flat fields), `lens/traces.tsx` tag chips use `Badge`, `lens/datasets.tsx` items use `DataTable`, and the `lens/ask.tsx` answer heading matches the reference typography. New `pages/lens.test.tsx` (Overview KPI + Explore) + updated datasets/analytics tests; 43 lens+overview+smoke tests + `tsc` + `eslint` green. Behavior unchanged (same BFF, same sub-page routes); pure frontend standardization, no new dependency.
- **M0 J-02 Register — `/v1/auth/me` lazily provisions the free starter plan when missing (T1.3).** Closes the end-to-end gap: `ProvisionStarter` previously fired only on `/billing` access, so a brand-new tenant had no subscription row and the completing screen's `/me` poll stayed `pending` forever. `MeHandler` now takes the `billing.Provisioner` (via a local `starterProvisioner` DIP interface) and, on `sql.ErrNoRows`, lazily creates the starter subscription (idempotent `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`) — mirroring the existing `/billing` lazy-ensure idiom — so the first poll resolves to `active`/`starter` and the register flow redirects immediately. Fail-safe: a failed provision stays `pending` (next poll retries), never a 500; billing-off (nil provisioner) reports `pending`. Wired nil-safe in `cmd/main.go` (typed-nil guard). 2 new sqlmock tests (lazy-provisions-starter, provision-fails-stays-pending); full `go test ./...` green.
- **M0 J-02 Register — post-registration "completing" screen + provisioning poll (T2.1 + T3.1).** New `useProvisioningStatus` hook polls the BFF `GET /v1/auth/me` every 2s **only while** provisioning is `pending`, stopping on any terminal state (`active` / `unknown` / absent) — the cadence is an extracted pure function (`provisioningRefetchInterval`) unit-tested without timers. New `RegisterCompletingScreen` (a "Setting up your workspace" spinner) calls `onReady` once provisioning resolves; wired into `register.tsx` so a detected session hands off to it instead of redirecting immediately — a brand-new tenant lands on the app only after its bootstrap is ready, while an already-provisioned session resolves on the first poll (no delay, transparent pass-through). Consumes the T1.2 `/me` snapshot (full wiring triad: backend → hook → screen → page). 5 hook tests + 4 component tests + 1 page wiring test (session → completing screen, form hidden); vitest + tsc + eslint green.
- **M0 J-02 Register — `GET /v1/auth/me` carries a tenant provisioning snapshot (T1.2).** The register "completing" screen (post-OIDC) needs to poll whether the new tenant's after-registration bootstrap has finished before redirecting to `/projects`. `MeHandler` now takes the BFF Postgres handle and, alongside the identity fields, returns `provisioning` (`active` | `pending` | `unknown`), `plan`, and `subscription_status`, derived from the latest `subscriptions` row (the row `billing.Provisioner.ProvisionStarter` writes during bootstrap — its presence is the readiness signal). Fail-clear + resilient: `sql.ErrNoRows` → `pending`; a DB error → `unknown` with a logged error (the identity endpoint NEVER 500s on a billing-lookup failure); a nil DB → provisioning fields omitted. Additive to the `/me` contract (Rule 5 — new fields only). 5 sqlmock unit tests (active / pending / db-error-degrade / nil-db-omit / 401); `go build ./...` + `go vet` + full `go test ./...` green. Wires the frontend `useWhoami` provisioning poll (T2.2, next).
- **theo-lens management dashboard UI (M44, frontend half).** New `dashboard/src/pages/lens/` section (8 pages) mirroring the theo-rag/theo-memory dashboard pattern (Rule 9): **Traces** (read list + create/delete tags & annotations — the human-in-the-loop metadata; the trace body stays read-only), **Sessions** (read-only replay), **Datasets** (list/create + item remove), **Evaluators** (list/create/**run**/delete), **Guards** (list/create/delete), **Agents** (read-only derived catalog), **Ask** (the M33 copilot, read-scope), **Analytics** (observability + cost). Read via `useQuery`, mutate via `useMutation` + `invalidateQueries`, destructive actions gated behind `ConfirmDialog`; consumed through `cloudFetch` (injects the browser JWT + an Idempotency-Key). Registered in `app.tsx` (lazy + `<Boundaried>` routes) + `app-sidebar.tsx` (a `Lens` drill-down section). 15 component tests (list/empty/create/run/delete + ConfirmDialog gating + read-only pages assert zero mutating calls) + the pages smoke test green; `tsc --noEmit` + `eslint src/pages/lens` clean.
- **theo-lens management dashboard read+write BFF (M44, backend half).** Extends the M41 read-only `/v1/dashboard/lens/*` BFF to **read+write+delete** per-tenant so a human manages theo-lens from the browser: `datasets` (list/detail/create/add-item/delete-item), `evaluators` (list/create/run/delete), `guards` (list/create/delete/breaches), and trace **metadata** — tags + annotations (create/delete). `internal/thelens/data_client.go` gains a `doRaw` write dispatch (mirrors `internal/rag/data_client.go`: fail-closed per-tenant Bearer, pass-through `json.RawMessage`, upstream ≥400 → error, bearer never leaked) + 18 read/write methods pinned by a **real-schema contract test** to theo-lens's actual field names + native paths (`POST /v1/datasets {name}`, `POST /v1/evaluators/:id/run`, tag delete via `?tag=` query, annotation delete via `?name=&spanId=&annotatorKind=`, copilot at `POST /v1/traces/:id/ask`). `internal/routes/lens_dashboard.go` gains the write handlers (each fail-closed 401 on no tenant, 502 on upstream error) + the exported `MountLensDashboard` route table. The dashboard resolver now mints a `lens:read+write+delete` key (ADR-1 — one resolver; per-verb least privilege enforced at theo-lens). **ADR-3 route-set invariant** (`cmd/main_test.go` + `lens_dashboard_test.go`): NO mutating verb on the trace/session **body** or on `/agents`; tags/annotations ARE writable metadata; `replay-sessions` is not exposed (ADR-2 — a north-star event, not managed data). The write path is proven through the real Traefik edge in M44 T2.1 (hermetic e2e). `go build`/`go vet`/`go test ./internal/routes ./internal/thelens ./cmd` green; gofmt clean.
- **theo-lens dashboard read BFF (M41).** Read-only `/v1/dashboard/lens/*` routes (`traces`, `sessions`, `observability`, `cost`) so the central Theo dashboard renders aggregated, workspace-scoped lens data via a per-tenant `lens:read` key minted from the browser JWT (least privilege — a compromised session can never write/delete through the BFF; the write half is M44). New `internal/thelens/data_client.go` (a thin Model-B read proxy: no standing credential, a per-request per-tenant Bearer, fail-closed, pass-through `json.RawMessage`) + `internal/routes/lens_dashboard.go` (fail-closed 401 on no tenant, read-only handler). `buildLensForwardAuth` now also returns the broker so the same mint cache serves the edge ForwardAuth handler AND the dashboard resolver (mirrors rag). Wired behind `requireJWT`; nil/unmounted when `LENS_BASE_URL` is unset. The **real-schema contract test** pins theo-lens's actual native paths (observability under `/v1/metrics`, cost under `/v1/analytics` — not `/v1/observability`/`/v1/cost`; the rag-M22 502 class), and a route-set test asserts the mount is GET-only. `go test ./... -race` + `go vet` green.
- **Hermetic theo-lens edge ForwardAuth e2e (M40 — KEYSTONE).** The non-negotiable platform-level proof of invariant #6 for lens: a real theo-lens container (built from pinned sibling source, self-migrating on boot, `THEOLENS_REQUIRE_CREDENTIAL=true`) behind the real Traefik edge + real ForwardAuth (the M38 `/internal/authz/forward-lens` handler) + real broker, proving END-TO-END through the edge that: two tenants are isolated (B creates a dataset, A cannot list it, B can), a minted `lens:read` key is **403 on a write verb** through Traefik, a no-key request is **401 at the edge** (no `Authorization` leaked), a direct-to-service no-key request is **401** (M13), and the RAW customer key does NOT resolve direct-to-service (minted ≠ customer). New `tests/e2e/edge_lens_forward_auth_e2e_test.go` (datasets CRUD canary, no LLM); `docker-compose.edge.e2e.yaml` gains `lens` + `pg-lens` (plain postgres, no migrate container — theo-lens migrates on boot); `traefik/dynamic.e2e.yml` gains `lens-edge` + `forward-auth-lens` + `lens-rewrite` → `http://lens:8080`; `run.sh` builds the pinned lens image, mints the lens platform-admin (flat `.secret`), and gates on lens edge-readiness. `make e2e-edge` green: 3 lens tests PASS alongside the memory + rag edge proofs (8 total, 0 fail).
- **Model-B theo-lens credential broker (M38).** The gateway can now provision per-tenant `theo_live_*` keys for the theo-lens data plane, mirroring the existing rag/promptly/memory brokers (Rule 9). New `internal/thelens` package: an `AdminClient` that holds the `lens:platform-admin` control-plane credential and mints via theo-lens's `POST /v1/admin/keys` (M22 — parsed as the FLAT `{id,scopes,secret}@200` shape, not theo-rag's nested envelope), plus a `Broker` with LRU + singleflight + fail-closed and least-privilege scope intersection (a `lens:read` consumer can never receive a write/delete key). Wired as a 4th `ForwardAuthHandler` at `/internal/authz/forward-lens` (required scope `lens:read`), constructed from `LENS_BASE_URL` + `LENS_PLATFORM_ADMIN_KEY` (base-url set ⇒ admin-key required in prod; boot refuses to start on broker error). The handler is nil (unregistered) unless `LENS_BASE_URL` is set — existing deployments are unchanged. The platform-admin key is held ONLY in `AdminClient`, never on the data path (NIST AC-5/6). The platform-level hermetic edge proof is M40 (theo-lens `ROADMAP-edge-integration.md`).


### Removed
- **Dashboard: fold the Observability `Analytics` sub-page into the `Overview`.** The `/observability/analytics` page duplicated the Overview's KPI strip (same Traces / Error-rate / Latency-p95 / Cost, same `/v1/dashboard/lens/{observability,cost}` BFF endpoints), so it was pure redundancy (YAGNI). Removed the `lens/analytics.tsx` page, its lazy route in `app.tsx`, the sidebar sub-nav item, and the Overview Explore-card that linked to it — the Overview already shows those KPIs. The observability+cost aggregation stays covered by the Overview's `lens.test.tsx`; the redundant analytics test was dropped. 12 lens+sidebar tests + `eslint` + dashboard `build` (`tsc -b && vite build`) green.


### Fixed
- **cloud-API: `go test ./cmd/` back to green — the M55 span-replay POST tripped the lens-dashboard read-only route-set invariant in the top-level test (theo-cloud#169).** `TestLensDashboard_ReadWriteRouteSet` (`cmd/main_test.go`) failed with `ADR-3 violation: mutating POST on read-only surface "/v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{traceID}/replay"`. Root cause: the M55 span-replay feature added `POST /v1/dashboard/lens/traces/{traceID}/replay` (`internal/routes/lens_dashboard.go` — a read-scope ephemeral provider re-run that **never persists to the immutable trace**) and correctly allowlisted `/replay` in the package-level route-set test (`internal/routes/lens_dashboard_test.go`), but the **duplicate `writableMeta` allowlist** in the top-level integration test was not updated in lockstep — so `go test ./internal/routes/` was green while `go test ./cmd/` was red, blocking any clean `develop → main` release. Fix: mirror the ratified precedent — add `/replay` to the `writableMeta` allowlist in `cmd/main_test.go` with a matching doc-comment, plus positive coverage asserting the sanctioned `POST …/replay` route is actually mounted. No production code changed (theo-lens M55's route is already correct); this aligns the top-level test's copy of the invariant with the sibling test. (#169)
- **cloud-API: session JWT TTL bumped 15 min → 24 h so a full dogfood-all sweep doesn't degrade mid-chain (dev-public cleanroom-up-live-test mandate).** `cmd/main.go` `NewSessionTokenIssuer(..., 15*time.Minute)` → `24*time.Hour`. The 15-min TTL was tighter than the wall-clock cost of a full `dogfood-all --live-only` sweep against a warm dev-public cluster (≈40-60 min), so JWTs minted at the start of a run were rejected as `AUTH_REQUIRED` past the ~15-min mark and every LIVE bash after that timestamp reported spurious FAILs (`no builds returned`, `HTTP 401 expected 404`, `other_envs_with_releases: None`) that misled the operator into chasing UI/data bugs instead of the real cause (expired credential). Pairs with `theo-contracts` v-next `Issue()` variadic + `SessionClaims.AccountID` field (companion commit `adbd67e` on the contracts side). Post-fix: JWT `exp-iat = 86400`; full dogfood-all completes without mid-chain re-auth.
- **cloud-API: `go test ./...` back to green — traces:read graduated to observability:* but its api-keys unit test still expected the reserved-503 (theo-cloud#166).** When re-verifying `origin/develop` on 2026-07-04, `TestCreateKey_ReservedScopeReturns503/traces:read` failed: the handler correctly returned **400 UNKNOWN_SCOPE** while the test expected **503 scope_not_yet_active**. Root cause: commit `7c1adc9` ("feat(auth)!: activate observability:* customer scope + rename edge scope lens:* → observability:*") graduated `traces:*` from the reserved set at `internal/routes/api_keys.go` and **renamed** it to `observability:*` in `validScopes` (ACTIVE), but the mirror table at `tests/unit/api_keys_create_test.go` still listed `traces:read` in the reserved-cases slice — and the graduated form `observability:read` had **zero positive coverage** at the create-key handler layer (E2E edge tests seed keys via SQL, they do not exercise `POST /v1/api-keys`). Fix (TDD-first): (1) drop `traces:read` from the reserved-cases slice with a linking comment pointing at 7c1adc9 + the new positive test; (2) add `TestCreateKey_ObservabilityScopesAccepted` — a table test over `observability:{read,write,delete,admin}` asserting 200 with the sqlmock `INSERT INTO theo_api_keys` matcher — so the graduation is protected against silent regression. `go test ./... -short` rc=0 (20 packages OK, `tests/unit` 6 reserved subtests + 4 new observability subtests PASS). No production-code change.
- **Dashboard: `npm run lint` + `npm test` back to green (theo-cloud#163, theo-cloud#164).** Two independent regressions surfaced when re-verifying `origin/develop` on 2026-07-04: (a) ESLint reported 617 errors on the vendored theo-lens design-system bundle at `docs/theo-lens/_ds/theoui-violet-forge-*/_ds_bundle.js` (added by d966371 as a generated artifact — `/* @ds-bundle: … */` header, drags browser+node globals `require`/`window`/`document`/`console`/`__REACT_DEVTOOLS_GLOBAL_HOOK__`) because `eslint.config.js` `ignores` did not include `docs/**` (the pattern already excludes other vendored/generated trees); (b) `cross-cuts-p4.test.tsx` failed with `[vitest] No "ApiError" export is defined on the "@/lib/api" mock` because a J-13 change wired `useInvoices` → `q.error instanceof ApiError` while the Plan-4 test's `vi.mock('@/lib/api', …)` still returned only `cloudFetch`/`engineFetch`/`getApiBase`, so the `InvoicesCard` under `BillingPage` crashed and the memory card never rendered. Fix (a): add `docs/**` to the ESLint `ignores` list. Fix (b): rewrite the mock with vitest's recommended `async (importOriginal) => ({ ...actual, … })` partial-mock pattern so `ApiError` (and any other genuine export the page transitively touches) is preserved while only the two fetchers are overridden. Full dashboard suite regreens: 179 files / 1268 passed / 8 skipped; `npx eslint .` rc=0; `npx tsc --noEmit` rc=0.
- **Dashboard: offer only the webhook events that actually fire (theo-cloud#153).** The `/settings/webhooks` registration UI listed four subscribable events (`deploy.succeeded`, `deploy.failed`, `build.succeeded`, `build.failed`) but the engine emits only the two `deploy.*` events (release→ACTIVE + build-execution failure). A customer subscribing **only** to `build.succeeded`/`build.failed` would never receive a delivery — a silent honesty gap (Rule 3). Trimmed `AVAILABLE_EVENTS` to the emitted set (`deploy.succeeded`, `deploy.failed`); the `build.*` taxonomy (whether `deploy.failed` subsumes `build.failed`, or the build outcome is a distinct emitted event) is tracked in #153 for a future ADR. `tsc`/`vitest` (5/5) green.

- **Memory webhook worker: a delivery orphaned by a worker crash is now reclaimed, not silently lost forever (theo-cloud#160).** `claimDue` marked a row `delivering` but there was **no reaper**, and `claimDue` only selects `pending`/`failed` — so a worker crash between claim and `recordResult` left the row `delivering` **permanently** (never retried, never dead-lettered, the customer's memory-webhook event silently dropped). Fix (no migration): `claimDue` stamps a lease deadline on `next_attempt_at` (which it never reads for a `delivering` row), and a new `reapStaleDeliveries` (called each `tick`) reclaims rows past their lease as a real failed attempt — counted, backed off via `NextBackoff`, dead-lettered at `MaxAttempts` (DRY with `recordResult`). The claim batch is clamped to `MaxInFlight` (was 50 vs 10) so a live dispatch never outlives its lease — the reaper only ever touches genuinely-crashed orphans, making a lost-update race structurally unreachable without a `claimed_at` column. 3 new real-Postgres tests (stale orphan reclaimed + attempt counted + backed off; fresh lease not reaped; dead-letters at cap; claim stamps a future lease deadline); `go build`/`vet` + full package green. Backports the lease+reaper the workspace worker already had.
- **J-18 KPI displays (SignalGrid + KPIStripStatus) render non-finite metrics as "—" muted, not "NaN%" (theo-cloud#158).** `fmtPercent`/`fmtMs`/`fmtNumber` guarded `typeof v !== 'number'` — but `typeof NaN === 'number'`, so a NaN/Inf metric (e.g. an `error_rate` `0/0` for an idle tenant) rendered `"NaN%"`/`"NaNms"`/`"NaN"`, contradicting the component's own honest-empty-state contract (null/undefined → `—`). Both J-18 KPI components' numeric formatters + tone functions now require a finite number (`typeof … === 'number' && Number.isFinite`), so non-finite values honour the same `—` empty state. Defense-in-depth belt to theo#180 (which drops NaN at the Prometheus-client boundary API-side). New `signal-grid.test.tsx` case renders `error_rate: NaN` + `p95_latency: Infinity` + `rps: -Infinity` and asserts no `NaN`/`Infinity` token leaks + `—` renders; `vitest` (15/15) + `tsc` + `eslint` green. Pure formatter guard, no contract change.
- **Onboarding panel tests — align stale label queries to the shipped `#163` a11y badge.** The QA-sprint a11y fix (`3a59656`, WCAG 2.5.3 label-in-name) changed the collapsed FAB accessible name to `"{done}/{total} — Get your app live. Click to expand."`, but 9 `onboarding-panel.test.tsx` queries still looked for the old `/N of 5 done/` label and were silently red. Re-pointed them at the shipped `/get your app live/i` label — pure test-to-reality alignment, zero production change. Onboarding panel back to 10/10 green.
- **M0 J-02 Register — completing screen bounded wait (EC-1 + EC-2).** A cross-validation review surfaced that `RegisterCompletingScreen` had an unbounded `pending` wait — in the narrow window where the starter-plan lazy INSERT keeps failing, `/me` returns `pending` on every poll and the user was stuck on an infinite "Setting up your workspace" spinner. Added a `maxWaitMs` (default 60s) bounded wait: past the deadline while still pending, the screen surfaces a "This is taking longer than expected" warning with a **Continue to dashboard** escape + support contact (absorbs the plan's EC-1 stuck/failed escape + EC-2 >60s warning). 3 new fake-timer tests; vitest 20 (screen+page+hook) + tsc + eslint green.
- **M0 J-01 — `login.tsx` LoC-budget guard (≤500).** New `login-loc-budget.test.ts` vitest asserts the page stays ≤500 lines after the Phase-2 component extraction (LoginErrorBanner / CookieBlockedAlert / NetworkRetryButton + return-to/safe-redirect helpers) — a refactor that re-inlines error/redirect logic back into the page fails here (T3.1 EC-4). Green.
- **M0 J-03 Verification — `ResendCooldownButton` (persisted 60s cooldown) + Playwright e2e.** The verification page's "Didn't receive the email? Resend" affordance had no rate-limit, so a user could spam Kratos's courier by clicking (or reloading and clicking). New `dashboard/src/components/verification/resend-cooldown-button.tsx`: a reusable button that persists the cooldown deadline (epoch ms) in `localStorage`, so the countdown survives a reload/remount and re-enables itself when it elapses — 5 vitest cases (idle, click-starts-persisted-cooldown, restore-from-storage-on-mount, count-down-and-reenable, expired-deadline-idle) via deterministic fake timers. Wired into `verification.tsx` (input state) + 1 page integration test. J-03 T1.2 (paste distribution) and T1.3 (auto-submit on 6 digits) are already satisfied natively by the `@usetheo/ui` `PinInput` primitive (its docstring: "Paste handling fills all slots from clipboard"; `onComplete` wired to submit) — no wrapper built (Rule 9 / YAGNI). New `dashboard/e2e/verification.spec.ts` (2 scenarios: code-field + enabled resend renders; a persisted cooldown deadline renders the button disabled with a live countdown) — both PASS via the `__THEO_MOCK_LOGGED_OUT` harness affordance. tsc + eslint clean; vitest for the touched areas green.
- **M0 J-01 Login e2e — Playwright auth-page harness repaired + `dashboard/e2e/login.spec.ts` (3 scenarios) green.** The e2e webServer runs mocks logged-IN (`VITE_USE_MOCKS=true`), so the dev-mocks `window.fetch` wrapper intercepts every `/self-service/*` request at the JS layer and `getMockSession()` returns a logged-in "Demo Operator" session — which silently redirected EVERY auth-page e2e (login/register/verification/recovery/device) to the mock dashboard, and left `page.route()` flow mocks dead (unreachable). Added two dev-only per-page runtime overrides to the mock layer (dead-code-eliminated in prod, gated by `mocksEnabled()`): `window.__THEO_MOCK_LOGGED_OUT` (render the auth page logged-out instead of redirecting to `/`) and `window.__THEO_MOCK_FLOW_ERROR` (inject a `ui.messages` error into the mocked login flow so the error-render path runs end-to-end in the browser). New `dashboard/e2e/login.spec.ts`: 3 scenarios — OAuth-denial "We couldn't sign you in." Alert (not a silent redirect), and `?provider=github|google` autofocus + visual ring — all PASS. `tsc` + `eslint` + vitest (34 pass / 8 skip) regression-free.


### Security
- **Close a live SSRF in the memory-webhook delivery worker (CRITICAL, #148).** The delivery worker POSTs to a customer-registered URL; it used a default `http.Client` with no egress control, so a tenant could register a webhook targeting an internal address (cloud metadata `169.254.169.254`, RFC1918, loopback) and make the cloud process issue the request from inside the VPC — worst case metadata → IAM credentials, with the response body persisted on the delivery row as an exfil channel. New shared **`internal/safehttp`** client validates the **resolved destination IP at socket-connect time** (`net.Dialer.Control` rejects loopback / link-local incl. metadata / RFC1918 / ULA / unspecified / multicast — defeats DNS rebinding) and **does not follow redirects** (`CheckRedirect → ErrUseLastResponse`, so a 3xx to an internal `Location` can't bypass the check). Injected into the memory worker's default-client path. Found during the J-16 slice 3 governance review (MEET-2026-07-03-005); the forthcoming workspace/deploy webhook worker reuses the same guard rather than mirroring the gap (DRY). 4 guard tests (IP deny-table incl. metadata + rebinding-safe connect-time control + zero-socket-to-loopback + no-follow-redirects) + 1 worker regression test proving the default client rejects a loopback target with zero connections.

### Fixed
- **RAG dashboard document ingest was broken — BFF sent `text` where theo-rag requires `content` (M21 dogfood, closes the upload journey).** Live dogfood of the M21 RAG dashboard (real Chrome, local `up-local` stack) found `POST /v1/dashboard/rag/documents` → `502 UPSTREAM_ERROR`: theo-rag's `POST /v1/documents/raw` returned `422 {"error":"invalid_content","detail":"content must be a string"}` because `internal/rag/data_client.go:IngestDocument` marshaled `IngestInput{Text}` (json `text`) directly as the upstream body, but theo-rag's contract requires `content` (+ a non-empty `name`). Fixed by mapping to a dedicated `ragRawIngestBody{collection_id, content, name}` (defaulting an empty name to `untitled`) so the two wire contracts never drift. The M21 BFF contract tests missed this because they stubbed the data client and never exercised theo-rag's real `/v1/documents/raw` schema — added `TestRagDataClient_IngestDocument_SendsContentField` + `_DefaultsEmptyName` regression tests. Re-verified live: upload → async status PENDING → READY (worker embed) → Ask returns a cited answer. (`internal/rag/data_client.go`, `internal/rag/data_client_test.go`)
- **Dashboard `Live envs` metric prefere `active_env_count` sobre `production_env_count` (closes Red Team theo-cloud#134 MAJOR).** Root cause: `project-detail.tsx:156` + `projects.tsx:164` derivavam a métrica via `production_env_count ?? environment_count`, mas `production_env_count` conta apenas `env_targets.kind='permanent'` — para theokit-example que servia produção via **preview env** com release ACTIVE, essa contagem retornava 0 enquanto o Live URL renderizado na mesma tela apontava para um ambiente ativo. Sintoma: rótulo enganoso "Live envs: 0" contradizendo o Live URL exposto. Fix: (a) `dashboard/src/lib/api/types.ts` — novo campo opcional `active_env_count?: number` no `Project` type; (b) `dashboard/src/pages/project-detail.tsx:156` — fallback chain agora `active_env_count ?? production_env_count ?? environment_count ?? (environment ? 1 : 0)`; (c) `dashboard/src/pages/projects.tsx:164` — mesma fallback chain para consistência cross-view (fecha classe de bug #77 / #124). Engine-side companion em `theo/api/internal/domain/types.go` + `theo/api/internal/db/queries/projects.sql` (LATERAL JOIN `env_targets` com release ACTIVE) surface `active_env_count` no wire response. Vitest regression-protector: `test_project_detail_uses_active_env_count_for_live_envs_metric (#134)` em `project-detail.test.tsx` (5/5 GREEN). Requires rebuild + push + rollout do theo-api + theo-cloud dashboard image para o Red Team ver o fix (per `feedback_always_rebuild_push_rollout_for_red_team_2026_06_27`). Per `feedback_issue_fix_requires_failing_e2e_test_2026_06_25` INQUEBRÁVEL. (`dashboard/src/lib/api/types.ts`, `dashboard/src/pages/project-detail.tsx`, `dashboard/src/pages/projects.tsx`, `dashboard/src/pages/project-detail.test.tsx`)
